# Human Capability Development Principles Framework

HCD is a pattern language for deriving human capability demand from later Work, designing and sustaining development arrangements, and judging human contribution, transfer and retention under stated task, support and time conditions.

> **Author:** Anatoly Levenchuk with AI-assisted development and review.
> **Version:** 5 September 2026
> **Status:** Eternal alpha: used in working development projects and open to continuing correction.
> **Publication:** [FPF repository](https://github.com/ailev/FPF). [Citation](#citation).

# Table of Contents

Use the Readme when you have a working difficulty but do not yet know the direct pattern. Use this Table of Contents when you already know the PatternID. The seventeen pattern bodies contain the authoritative Methods.

`HCD.*` is the PatternID namespace. The numbers are stable addresses, not a Work sequence; every HCD.1–HCD.17 body is available in this first edition.

# Human Capability Development Principles Framework Readme

## Practical entries

This **Human Capability Development Principles Framework — Seventeen-pattern first edition** helps a person, manager, human-capability practitioner, provider or another accountable decision owner derive human demand, diagnose a limiting target, compare a profile, compose and compare development programmes, select a development Method, design representative practice, arrange support, conduct and progress focused practice, judge performance evidence, test unfamiliar transfer and retention, revise an affected development arrangement, sustain continuing development, refresh the HCD Method repertoire, or deliberately continue and change an HCD cultural relation.

All patterns HCD.1–HCD.17 are available here. This seventeen-pattern first edition gives their authoritative Methods, worked cases, source limits, checks and return conditions in the bodies. The Readme provides discovery, connected applications, source qualification and publication boundaries; it does not reconstruct the body Methods.
The entries below are selected examples, not a catalogue or coverage boundary. Bring the actual question. If no example fits, use the Table of Contents or direct patterns to find the relevant contribution. The entries are independent situation-first starts, not stages in a lifecycle.

### HCD-DEMAND - Derive one person's demand from representative later Work

- **Situation:** A job title, competency list, course request, platform score, MethodDescription, or AI rollout is being treated as proof of what one person must be able to do.
- **Question:** What must this exact human contribute in which representative later Work, under which conditions, and what evidence makes that demand current?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** A human capability-demand account, prospective demand hypothesis, outside-HCD return, or `demand-evidence-blocked` result naming each absent consumed value and retry predicate.
- **Start with:** [HCD.1](#hcd-1).
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the demand disposition. Do not select a programme. Return missing Method, specialist, assignment, authority, access, environment, tool, organization, clinical, or target-domain results to their direct owners.

### HCD-TARGET - Distinguish a human limitation from a non-training cause

- **Situation:** Performance is inadequate or unstable and training, practice, coaching, motivation work, or treatment is being proposed before decision-changing rivals have been compared.
- **Question:** Which smallest changeable human limitation is supported, or which non-training branch owns the next result?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** A qualified intervention target with evidence, any still-plausible rival, protected conditions, intended observation, specialist boundary, and reopen condition; or a non-training return naming the actual owner or missing result, blocked HCD decision, safe boundary, and retry condition.
- **Start with:** [HCD.3](#hcd-3) only when the same-holder HCD.1 account or an equivalent qualified later-Work input is recoverable.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the target or non-training result. Select no intervention, programme, treatment, surveillance, assignment, or employment action.

### HCD-PROFILE - Balance simultaneous capability claims without inventing mastery

- **Situation:** A skills matrix, level, mastery score, course path, Method hierarchy, or role profile hides which contributions are supported, which gap matters, and what must not be traded away.
- **Question:** What should be preserved, investigated, deferred, narrowed, or returned before one profile priority can be chosen?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** A claim-by-claim condition-qualified profile, trade-offs, priority characteristics, and recommendation or unresolved question; a bounded `C.11` choice obtained by applying its rule to the required current inputs and conditions; or `profile-decision-blocked` with the exact missing value and retry condition.
- **Start with:** [HCD.4](#hcd-4) only when the same-holder HCD.1 scope/result frame or an equivalent qualified demand account is recoverable.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the comparison, recommendation, lawful bounded choice, or exact blocker. Do not turn the profile into a programme choice, authority, commitment, ongoing-Work steering, or transformation evidence.

### HCD-PROGRAMME - Compare feasible development arrangements for later Work

- **Situation:** A named person has later Work, a qualified target or profile, and finite time and support, yet a syllabus, course label or single proposal is being treated as the programme. Or a learning-product author is designing for a future audience before a particular learner is known.
- **Question:** Which materially different feasible development arrangements can supply the needed contributions, and how do their complete benefits, burdens, support dependence and evidence needs compare?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** For a known person, a condition-qualified programme comparison or exact missing input; for an unknown future learner, a learning-product design return rather than a personal recommendation.
- **Start with:** [HCD.2](#hcd-2), using same-holder HCD.1, HCD.3 and HCD.4 values or qualified equivalents when a personal comparison is current.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the comparison or design return. Programme choice, commitment, launch, performed learning and effect remain separate.

### HCD-METHOD - Select a development Method for the actual target

- **Situation:** Coaching, deliberate practice, retrieval, interleaving, motivation work, AI tutoring, or another intervention family is being selected by familiarity or label.
- **Question:** Which Method plausibly fits the target action, starting attempt, mechanism, task, learner, provider, risk and evidence need?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** A bounded Method selection or shortlist with expected action, support and observation; an exact non-training return; or the missing input needed to decide.
- **Start with:** [HCD.5](#hcd-5), after the qualified target and relevant starting evidence are recoverable.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at Method fit. A selection is not evidence that the Method or programme is effective.

### HCD-SUPPORT - Establish the support that will actually be available

- **Situation:** A teacher, mentor, specialist, platform, tool, AI system or block of hours is named, but the exact contribution, competence, access, capacity, checking or fallback is not established.
- **Question:** Which provider, source, tool, AI, environment and human-check contributions are required, who or what can supply each one, and will they operate at the needed time and load?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** An enabled support arrangement with actual access and fallback, or the exact missing contribution. HCD.8 is entered only when obtaining or building that missing contribution is selected.
- **Start with:** [HCD.7](#hcd-7), from a selected programme, Method, practice task or other bounded development use.
- **Stop or return:** Availability is not service operation, learner performance or a development effect. Return unsupported provider, access, authority, privacy, safety or capacity values to their owners.

### HCD-PRACTICE - Conduct a focused attempt, correction and retry

- **Situation:** “Hours practised”, a score, a model answer, or “feedback was given” hides the person's first attempt, actual help, correction and changed retry.
- **Question:** What did the person first attempt, which information-bearing feedback arrived, what did the person change, what happened on the targeted retry, and what should be tried next?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** A focused-practice episode preserving first response, support, criterion-bearing feedback, learner correction, retry, unresolved error and next focus.
- **Start with:** [HCD.9](#hcd-9), using a target action, representative task, selected Method and actually available support.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the episode result. Use HCD.10 when the next question is variation, spacing, retrieval, source, interleaving, support or challenge across episodes; use HCD.11–HCD.13 for stronger performance, transfer or retention claims.

### HCD-PERFORMANCE - Judge the person's contribution, not only the submitted answer

- **Situation:** A grade, document, helped answer or successful task is being used as evidence that a person can do later work.
- **Question:** What did this person demonstrably contribute under the required conditions, and what bounded claim does that support?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** A capability-evidence account separating observations, help, material errors and inference; or a missing observation, assessment defect or criterion to obtain.
- **Start with:** [HCD.11](#hcd-11), using a compatible HCD.1 demand frame or equivalent qualified input.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at evidence sufficient for the stated use. Return missing domain criteria, inaccessible tasks and stronger assurance or authority questions to their actual owners.

### HCD-TRANSFER - Find what survives a meaningful change in work

- **Situation:** Familiar success is being relied upon in work whose task, data, tool, setting or coordination has materially changed.
- **Question:** Which demonstrated contribution survives the unfamiliar change, and what still requires adaptation or help?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** A bounded transfer account preserving first and corrected attempts, justified action and refusal, tested variation and untested limits.
- **Start with:** [HCD.12](#hcd-12), using prior qualified performance from HCD.11 or equivalent evidence.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the supported transfer or adaptation result. Return an invalid task or missing prerequisite; obtain a fresh uncued observation before strengthening a cue-dependent claim. Keep new-source learning separate.

### HCD-RETENTION - Separate delayed performance from support dependence

- **Situation:** Earlier success is being relied upon after a delay, or the person now performs differently with changed support.
- **Question:** What remains demonstrable after this interval and practice history, and what depends on the support?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** Condition-qualified retention or support-dependence findings, with a separately justified independent-core result when needed and access or apparent-loss distinctions only where evidence supports them.
- **Start with:** [HCD.13](#hcd-13), using qualified earlier performance of the same contribution by the same person.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the smallest interpretable comparison. Return a known access failure to its owner; leave delay versus withdrawal unresolved if only their combined condition was observed. Obtain resources for any extra follow-up.

### HCD-REVISION - Change the part of development that evidence calls into question

- **Situation:** An assessment changes an assumption behind the person's tasks, practice, feedback, support or provider arrangement.
- **Question:** Which smallest justified change can improve the affected contribution while preserving what already works?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** An executable revision hypothesis with a fresh test, cost, protected conditions and retain/adapt/abandon rules; or the exact evidence, professional Method, resource or authority input to obtain.
- **Start with:** [HCD.14](#hcd-14), using a compatible HCD.11, HCD.12 or HCD.13 result; all three are not required.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the proposal or the bounded authorized change and its observed result. Return diagnosis to HCD.3 when needed, and keep the person's direction, programme-wide reconstruction and causal effectiveness as separate questions.

### HCD-CONTINUING - Keep development responsive after the programme ends

- **Situation:** One person's later Work, tool, provider, qualification condition or evidence has changed after an initial programme, while recurring activity is being treated as proof that capability remains current.
- **Question:** Which continuing arrangement should reopen from this Work or evidence change, and what can remain protected?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** A holder-specific arrangement with decision-bearing triggers, practice and support contributions, evidence and reassessment, burden, owners, authority, re-entry and stops; or the exact missing result and retry condition.
- **Start with:** [HCD.15](#hcd-15), using a compatible HCD.14 result or equivalent qualified evidence.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the arrangement or blocker. A calendar, participation record, authorized plan, performed development Work, later Work, achieved capability and causal effect remain different results.

### HCD-REPERTOIRE - Change one HCD Method claim without rebuilding the repertoire

- **Situation:** A learner population, later-Work condition, provider, tool or AI behavior, transfer or retention observation, safety boundary, source claim or serious alternative can change one HCD Method use.
- **Question:** Which exact admitted Method or status-preserved candidate and human-development claim should be adopted, revised, branched, retired, or probed for this bounded repertoire use?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** A current HCD repertoire slice with exact status, evidence conditions, alternatives, one bounded disposition, gaps, probe and reopen trigger.
- **Start with:** [HCD.16](#hcd-16), after the Method or candidate status, receiving use and decision-changing claim are recoverable.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the HCD use disposition. Return admission, reusable semantics, variants, provenance, generic trial, fit, worth and introduction to Method Engineering; use G.5 or G.11 only when their stronger set or currentness question is live.

### HCD-CULTURE - Continue or change one observable development-culture relation

- **Situation:** Providers or practitioners say that a school, standard, syllabus, rollout, community, repository or value has become “the culture,” but the receiving relation and later consequence are unclear.
- **Question:** Which exact HCD practice value is generated, transmitted, received, recognized, selected, remembered, retained or lost in this bounded population, and what authorized observation can change the decision?
- **First useful result or honest blocker:** A truthful enacted-Method or candidate/practice account, one testable predicate, carriers and authority, two serious hypotheses, a bounded intervention, later observations, a separate capability consequence, and `continue`, `revise`, `branch`, `replace`, `stop` or `unknown`.
- **Start with:** [HCD.17](#hcd-17), from a bounded population, place and period and an honest Method/candidate branch.
- **Stop or return:** Stop at the bounded cultural decision or exact missing value. Publication, institutional selection, attendance, community activity, capability and causal effect do not fill one another.

# Preface

## Start from the contribution that later Work needs

Use HCD when a person, manager or development practitioner needs to improve or sustain a human contribution to later Work, and must decide what development is worth attempting under real time, access and support conditions. The question may arise before a programme, during a difficult practice attempt, after a successful assessment, or years later when the Work changes. A learning-product author can also use the framework while the future audience is still an assumption.

The recurring difficulty is connecting a development activity to the contribution it should change. A curriculum may cover every topic while leaving whole tasks out of reach. A good assisted answer may conceal the action the person still cannot supply. A once useful arrangement may become unsuitable after a tool, task or provider changes. HCD makes these differences actionable: identify the required contribution, compare a plausible development change, obtain the support it needs, and judge only what the subsequent observation can establish.

Begin with the decision now in front of you. State whose contribution matters, in what later Work, what is already known, and which result would change the next action. Then enter the relevant body through the [Practical entries](#practical-entries). The first useful result can be small: a demand hypothesis, two comparable task fragments, a corrected practice attempt, one qualified performance claim, or an exact missing contribution to obtain. A known person needs compatible personal evidence; an author without named learners can proceed directly to HCD.2's learning-product design return.

The main choices concern complete-task attainability, preservation of useful existing contributions, and the effort displaced by development. More practice, realism, independence, assessment or provider preparation can improve one condition while making the whole arrangement too burdensome or changing the task being judged. Select these contributions for the live question and compare their combined demands. The [programme and instrument application](#application-return-to-a-programme-and-instrument-designer) makes that whole-arrangement constraint concrete.

## What the framework connects


Human Capability Development deliberately changes a human holder's capability for intended later Work. It is not every change in behaviour, every use of instruction, or every problem involving a person. A demand account states what later Work requires; a diagnostic target names what may need to change; a profile describes contribution claims; a programme sets out a bounded development arrangement; HCD.2 composes and compares programme candidates. Learning Work and clinical treatment are performed activities. Assessment provides observations and bounded inferences about performance, transfer, or retained capability. Programme, employment and other consequential decisions require their own authority.

The seventeen Methods keep one exact human holder or bounded HCD population, later Work, conditions, direct-owner results, finite resources, evidence, uncertainty and receiving decision aligned. Demand, diagnosis, profile, programme comparison, Method selection, practice-task design, support arrangement, bounded provider or environment development, focused practice, practice progression, performance assessment, transfer, retention, revision, continuing arrangement, HCD Method-repertoire change and cultural continuation can each be entered directly when their qualified inputs already exist. Their PatternIDs are stable addresses; file order and numbering do not prescribe Work order.

FPF supplies the transdisciplinary distinctions used here: human and other Systems, capability holders, Methods and MethodDescriptions, Work and results, evidence, architecture, decision objects, agency, authority, and precise plain language. Direct human-learning and behaviour sources supply bounded mechanism and evidence claims. Target-domain and specialist sources provide criteria and evidence for correctness, criticality, safety, clinical, legal, and employment questions. Qualified specialists exercise professional judgement, and authorized actors make the corresponding decisions.

## Human demand, capability evidence, development revision and transformation remain distinct

`HCD.1` derives what a human holder must contribute in later Work. It does not establish the holder's current capability. `HCD.3` identifies a supported limiting target or returns the question elsewhere. It does not select an intervention. `HCD.4` compares current and target claim rows and may support one bounded choice. It does not create a programme, learning trajectory, performed development Work, or transformation.

`HCD.2` composes and compares feasible programme candidates for one person's later Work, including complete contributions, burdens, support dependence and evidence needs; it does not choose or launch a programme. When no particular person is yet known, it returns the learning-product design result needed before a personal comparison. `HCD.5` selects a development Method for a qualified target and starting attempt; the selection is not an effectiveness finding. `HCD.6` designs a sufficiently whole, criterion-bearing practice task without treating surface realism as representation.

`HCD.7` establishes the provider, access, source, tool, AI, environment, human-check, capacity and fallback contributions required by a bounded use. `HCD.8` compares obtaining an exact missing contribution with building it, follows only the first blocking dependency, and requires an operating test. `HCD.9` preserves the first attempt, actual help, information-bearing feedback, learner correction, changed retry and next focus. `HCD.10` selects variation, spacing, retrieval, source, interleaving, support and challenge conditions separately across episodes.

HCD.11 returns observations and a bounded capability inference. HCD.12 tests a material unfamiliar variation. HCD.13 compares delayed performance and support conditions. HCD.14 uses an affected observation to revise a development assumption. A correct first attempt, a correction after feedback, learning from a newly supplied source, delayed performance, transfer, retention and a causal development effect are separately reportable.

HCD.15 configures a continuing arrangement that reopens from material Work or evidence changes; it is not a calendar, performed development Work or achieved capability. HCD.16 returns a bounded disposition for one exact admitted Method or status-preserved candidate and human-development claim; it neither admits nor universally retires a Method. HCD.17 returns a bounded HCD cultural-continuation account and decision from one testable predicate, actual carriers and authority, competing hypotheses and later observations; a school, standard, carrier, event or community label is not the culture.

Distinguish instructional events, learner activity, learning outcomes, later transfer, and job performance, and use corresponding evidence for claims about each. A MethodDescription, course completion, self-report, platform prediction, assisted score, published schedule or reserved provider hours can inform a bounded question. A capability, transfer, retention or effect claim needs a justified inference from appropriate observations.

## Several contributions can be simultaneous

Perception, reasoning, bodily action, coordination, decision, evidence return, tool use, and specialist interfaces can all matter in one representative Work occurrence. The relationships among these contributions need not follow a course sequence, Method hierarchy, Work order, organization chart, or dashboard.

A profile and programme comparison therefore preserve claim identity, conditions, support dependence, uncertainty, critical-error boundaries, trade-offs and finite burdens. A scalar is secondary and admissible only when the receiving decision loses none of those decision-bearing values.

Describe the contribution required in the person's work role and the conditions under which it is needed. The human capability holder is the person who fills that role. The same person can review evidence, coordinate a specialist and make a bounded proposal in the same Work, or fill different roles in different Work. HCD.4 therefore compares that person's contribution claims and their interfaces. A role description can help derive demand through HCD.1; personal starting evidence is still needed for diagnosis or a personal programme comparison.

Several people's development can support an organization's work, but an organizational result also depends on assignments, coordination, resources and other system contributions. Keep each person's evidence identifiable and take organization-holder capability or organization change to OCE and the relevant direct owners. A bounded cultural population is a different subject again: HCD.17 asks how one HCD practice value is transmitted, received, selected or retained among specified participants. It can examine that relation while individual capability consequences remain an HCD.11–HCD.13 question. A population becomes a capability holder only if the independent-System condition of E.23.CDI is separately established; HCD.1's population use otherwise preserves member or representative demand accounts.


## Pattern relations do not prescribe a lifecycle

`HCD.1` may supply evidence input to `HCD.3` and scope or result frames to `HCD.4`, `HCD.2` and `HCD.11`. HCD.3 and HCD.4 may supply target, starting-evidence, protected-strength and priority values to HCD.2 or HCD.5. Equivalent qualified inputs permit direct entry; no pattern result follows merely from an adjacent number.

A separately chosen programme or bounded use may call HCD.5 for Method selection, HCD.6 for representative tasks, HCD.7 for the support arrangement, and HCD.8 only for an exact missing contribution that must be obtained or built. HCD.5, HCD.6 and the enabled HCD.7/HCD.8 contributions may supply HCD.9. HCD.9 may supply practice history to HCD.10 or an attempt to HCD.11; HCD.10 designs later episodes but does not make their performance or retention findings.

HCD.11 can supply performance evidence to HCD.12 and HCD.13. Equivalent qualified observations permit direct entry. HCD.14 may use a compatible result from any one of HCD.11, HCD.12 or HCD.13; it need not wait for all of them. New evidence may also update HCD.3's diagnosis, HCD.4's profile, HCD.2's comparison or HCD.5's selection when it changes their question.

A compatible HCD.14 revision hypothesis may inform HCD.15's continuing-arrangement decision or HCD.17's cultural-variation comparison. Observed HCD.15 arrangement use and human-development evidence may inform HCD.16. A bounded HCD.16 repertoire disposition or status-preserved candidate variation may inform HCD.17. All four relations are conditional receiver-governed inputs: a missing, stale or differently scoped result requires a qualified direct substitute or an exact missing-result return, and no receiver waits merely because one PatternID is adjacent.

Use the smallest pattern whose result changes the current decision. A new observation reopens only the claims and receiving decisions whose evidence, conditions, or retry predicates it changes.

To combine uses, first name the result the combination should produce and the values each receiving Method actually needs. Reuse a result only when person or population, contribution, later Work, conditions and evidence window fit that receiving use. Then identify dependencies that require order, contributions used together, and competing demands on time or support. An available direct result can close a dependency immediately. HCD.8's obtain-or-build comparison is useful only when an exact missing contribution blocks the selected use. The [Direct result relations](#direct-result-relations) table locates these returns; the bodies explain how each result is produced.

For example, a practitioner can combine HCD.6, HCD.7 and HCD.9 for one supported practice task when the target and Method are already qualified. A fresh transfer miss can instead combine HCD.12 with HCD.14 to test a local revision, without rebuilding demand or waiting for a delayed observation. In a programme comparison, HCD.2 needs enough task, Method and support detail to compare complete candidates; fuller elaboration follows the separately selected use. These combinations share contributions, while their result, evidence and stopping question differ.

## Choose the scope and the evidence that change this use

The broad subject is deliberate human capability development. A personal programme comparison narrows it to finite changes for one person's later Work: HCD.2 compares complete candidate arrangements from that person's supported baseline. A future-audience learning-product design has a different result. It keeps audience assumptions, output tasks, alternatives, support and budgets explicit so that the author can develop a comparable product fragment; personal recommendation waits for the relevant person and evidence. This difference gives the author a useful direct start and identifies what individualization must later add.

Performance, unfamiliar transfer and retention narrow the evidence question in different ways. HCD.11 asks what the person contributed under specified conditions. HCD.12 asks what survives a material unfamiliar change to an already demonstrated contribution. HCD.13 compares earlier and delayed performance with the intervening practice and support conditions preserved. A claim about learning a newly supplied Method instead uses the declared new-source intention in HCD.12:4.5. These are useful distinct questions because the same correct answer can support different inferences, or fail to distinguish them.

The intended capability may include competent use of sources: a person may need to recognize a question, find a current rule, use a calculator and obtain a specialist result. Independence concerns the particular contribution being judged. If independent recognition is required, the person must select the relevant response before a teacher or AI supplies that selection; permitted references and accessibility support can remain. HCD.6 specifies the intended task conditions, HCD.7 establishes available support, and HCD.9/HCD.11 preserve the help actually received. HCD.12 and HCD.13 then interpret transfer or delay under those conditions. A delayed attempt with AI also withdrawn answers that combined condition; separate delay and support-dependence claims need an interpretable comparison.

Continuing development extends beyond a fixed course. HCD.15 connects changes in later Work or evidence to opportunities for practice, current sources, coaching, specialist access and reassessment under an agreed burden. A material change can reopen one contribution while the rest stays in ordinary use. Use HCD.14 for a local revision; enter HCD.15 when a continuing arrangement must respond to further changes. If the question concerns which human-development Method claim remains warranted for a population and use, HCD.16 compares that claim with serious alternatives. If it concerns how a practice continues among participants, HCD.17 tests the cultural relation. The [continuation application](#app-hcd-03---constructed-continuation-method-repertoire-and-culture) shows all three questions with their consequences kept separate.

These are different bounded uses of the available repertoire. They retain the same direct source, domain and authority boundaries. A narrower use is helpful when its specified person, audience, evidence question, or continuing-development object changes the action, result or stop.

## Architectural Rationale

HCD organizes the repertoire around working questions whose inputs and useful results can differ. Demand can be supported while diagnosis remains open; a practice correction can be useful before transfer is demonstrated; an operational assessor contribution can exist before any learner receives it. Separating these Methods lets a practitioner obtain the missing result and preserve established contributions. Combining them around later Work keeps that separation from becoming a collection of unrelated techniques. The cost is explicit attention to conditions and interfaces; reuse compatible results and keep the account only as detailed as the receiving decision requires.

Several serious approaches can supply part or all of a particular development arrangement. Compare them by the contribution needed in the same working situation.

| Approach | Practical strength and appropriate use | Why HCD keeps a broader repertoire, and when to prefer the approach directly |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A complex-skill programme designed through [4C/ID](https://www.4cid.org/about/) | Learning tasks connect supportive information for reasoning, procedural information during routine work, and additional part-task practice where automaticity needs more practice. This supplies a concrete architecture for a complex-skill curriculum. | HCD.2/.5/.6 adapt this task-and-support organization while retaining diagnosis, personal baselines, complete resource comparison and later evidence questions. A designer whose demand, audience and delivery conditions are settled can use 4C/ID directly for that programme. HCD remains useful when a human target, available support, continued development or a particular transfer/retention inference is still undecided. |
| [Cognitive apprenticeship and supervised participation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ijme.2020.100417) | Modelling and coaching can expose expert reasoning in situated tasks; guided participation provides access to work that a text alone may not supply. Prefer this form when competent supervision and suitable tasks can supply the target contribution within the consequence and resource limits. | HCD.5 compares the development contribution, HCD.7 establishes provider and access conditions, and HCD.8 addresses an exact missing contribution. Protected or infrequent work may require bounded simulations or separately prepared cases to make the needed observation possible. The trade-off is access to situated judgement versus supervision burden and the variation actually available; the label alone cannot settle the local comparison. |
| A learning product as the main development arrangement | A course, guide or interactive product can coordinate explanation, practice, pacing and feedback for a defined audience, and can make an already selected development design usable at scale. The Learning Product owner makes its concrete instructional and carrier decisions. | Use that product directly when it fits the person's task, entry evidence and support. HCD adds the comparison when fit, alternatives, finite burdens or later-Work evidence are unresolved, and provides a design return before particular learners are known. A course's audience assumptions remain design assumptions until the person-specific questions they matter to are answered. |
| A generic evaluation or testing framework | A framework for claims, tasks, observations and intended interpretations helps an assessor obtain evidence for a bounded judgement. HCD.11 adapts that contribution from evidence-centred design and the [Testing Standards](https://www.testingstandards.net/uploads/7/6/6/4/76643089/9780935302356.pdf). | HCD distinguishes the development decisions that use the judgement: diagnosis, local revision, unfamiliar transfer, delay, support dependence and continuing arrangement. Direct evaluation is enough when only its bounded judgement is needed. The wider HCD combination earns its cost when the same result requires a different development response, or when the proposed assessment would change the contribution being observed. |

The shared source line is therefore task- and evidence-sensitive human development. Whole tasks and expertise-sensitive support inform what to practise; feedback, retrieval, spacing and task similarity inform candidate practice changes; assessment sources qualify what can be inferred. Provider and workplace-learning sources connect learning opportunities to the conditions of participation and later Work. Each family contributes a bounded answer, not a proof that the whole local programme works. The [Source use](#source-use-and-currentness) account and each body's SoTA-Echoing section identify the specific adopted or adapted claim, population, task, support, horizon and limit. Reopen a choice when those conditions change or a serious alternative changes the next useful action; preserve unaffected source uses.

## Check the combination, learn from its result and revise

Recognition starts from the current difficulty and the first result that could change it. Stronger assurance begins when someone will rely on a claim of capability, transfer, retained performance, provider operation or effect. The selected body's criteria and recognition/assurance boundary govern that stronger question. At framework scope, check the connections that a local body cannot establish for the whole use:

- Does each consumed result concern the intended person or bounded population, contribution, later Work and conditions, with a clear distinction between observation, design and hypothesis?
- Does each required contribution have a feasible source, provider and time window, and does the whole combination fit learner and provider budgets including preparation, feedback, checking, retries and any delayed observation?
- Can the intended observation distinguish the human contribution from an answer supplied by help, and can its task and domain criteria support the declared inference?
- Do the receiving decisions preserve non-compensable errors, useful existing contributions, participation and access conditions, and the authority of their actual owners?
- Is the first result useful enough to stop or proceed now, with an observation or changed condition that can reopen only the affected claim?

The constructed applications show what those questions can change. In ImpactReview-17, a feasible probe is still worth doing before selecting a profile focus. In the L17 case, independent configuration checking survives one unfamiliar task while uncued resource adaptation remains unsupported; HCD.14 can propose a local practice change immediately. In ReleaseWork-R9, prospective demand remains useful while distinct missing observations block stronger same-holder conclusions. None of these constructed results supplies observations of a real learner.

The likely practical gain is a smaller and more informative next development action: compare one missing task fragment, obtain one assessor contribution, revise one cue-dependent exercise, or retain a working arrangement until a relevant condition changes. The costs include representative task preparation, competent judgement, time for correction and evidence that sometimes requires later access. Shared observations can reduce duplicate work only when their conditions fit each receiving inference. The programme application shows how apparently useful local activities can still exceed the common budget.

The engineering examples foreground explicit sources, configurable tools, auditable work products and consequential error checks. Work whose contribution is bodily, interpersonal or difficult to observe may need different representative tasks and qualified domain judgement. Learners also differ in starting evidence, language, accessibility needs, consent, available time and access to competent providers. Preserve those conditions before transferring a task or programme; keep required accessibility support in an independence test. The [clinical unlike case](#clinical-unlike-case---return-diagnosis-and-treatment-to-their-owner) demonstrates one material boundary where the specialist owns diagnosis and treatment.

Use HCD.14 to test the smallest development assumption changed by an observation, with an executable change, fresh task, protected contributions and retain/adapt/abandon conditions. Use HCD.15 when subsequent Work or evidence changes require a continuing arrangement. A contradicted source claim or serious new Method alternative can reopen the bounded HCD.16 repertoire question; a changed receiving-use or retention relation among participants can reopen HCD.17. Practical revision can proceed under a proportionate reversible hypothesis while a stronger causal-effect claim remains a separate evidence question.



# Part I — Human Capability Demand, Programme, Practice, Evidence, Continuation, Repertoire, and Culture

<a id="hcd-1"></a>
## HCD.1 — Derive Human Capability Demand from Representative Later Work

> **Type:** Human Capability Development practitioner method pattern


### HCD.1:0 - Use This When

Use this pattern when someone asks what a named person must become able to contribute in later Work and the available request is still expressed as a job title, competency list, course request, platform score, MethodDescription, or technology rollout. Begin with the exact human holder and representative later Work, not with a learning product.

The first useful result is a **human capability-demand account** recording which contributions that person needs to make in the named Work family, configuration, conditions, and horizon for the receiving decision. When the future Work is well framed but current evidence is not yet available, return a **prospective demand hypothesis** and the observations to obtain. When the question belongs to a clinical, target-domain, authority, organization, assignment, access, tool, or nonhuman-capability owner, return it there. When a conclusion consumes evidence that is absent, name each missing value and its retry predicate instead of calling the gap a learning need.

The practical gain is a demand that can guide diagnosis or profile comparison without pretending that training has been selected or that capability has already been observed. Use `HCD.3` when the demand is known and the question is which changeable human limitation, if any, is supported. Use `HCD.4` when the demand is known and simultaneous capability claims must be compared. A separate programme comparison (`HCD.2`) may use the account; deriving demand does not select a programme.

Do not use HCD.1 to prescribe diagnosis, treatment, curriculum, provider, employment action, safety judgement, release decision, or target-domain correctness. Those decisions remain with their direct owners.

### HCD.1:0.1 - Working Terms

| Name in this pattern | What it denotes |
| --- | --- |
| human holder | One identified human person whose capability demand is being considered. A role, cohort, roster, demographic category, or population is not that person. |
| later Work | A named family of future or recurring `U.Work` occurrences in which that person may contribute under stated conditions. |
| representative case | A case selected to examine a material variant of later Work, such as unfamiliar content, changed support, or a specialist interface. Its suitability depends on the conditions it represents and the conclusion for which it is used. |
| human capability demand | The contributions this person needs to be able to make under the named later-Work conditions. A demand account records those requirements; it does not establish the person's current capability. |
| demand claim | A statement that this person needs to be able to make a specified contribution under the named later-Work conditions, with the evidence for that requirement, uncertainty, and limits. |
| prospective demand hypothesis | A provisional account of future demand grounded in a supported future-Work frame. It states which demand claims remain provisional and which observations could qualify or revise them. |
| demand-evidence-blocked | A result that names each missing input, the conclusion it blocks, and the condition for retry. It also distinguishes available material from the missing input when that material could mislead the receiver. |
| outside-HCD return | A return of the current question and available evidence to the direct clinical, target-domain, authority, organization, assignment, access, tool, or other owner. |

The account records what later Work requires of the person. Evidence about present capability, causes, learning, or intervention effectiveness answers different questions and uses its own pattern.

### HCD.1:1 - Problem Frame

Human development is often commissioned from a visible carrier: a course catalogue, certification, role framework, AI platform, assessment dashboard, or manager's request. Those carriers can be useful, but none identifies by itself what a particular person must contribute in later Work.

Later Work is configured. The same named task can require different contributions when the product configuration, Method, information access, AI support, time, authority, specialist interface, or critical-error boundary changes. Assisted performance may also conceal which contribution belongs to the person and which belongs to a tool, another Agent, or an organization.

Demand therefore has to be recovered from representative Work and its intended result. Current observations are valuable, but future planning sometimes precedes an actual Work occurrence. The method must support honest prospective hypotheses without silently upgrading plans or demonstrations into current evidence.

### HCD.1:2 - Problem

A role-first or course-first description bundles unlike things. It can call a missing Method, inaccessible source, broken tool, absent specialist result, poor assignment, unsafe workload, or organizational constraint a human skill gap. It can also average across a population and then assign the average to one person.

The reverse mistake is to refuse any demand account until the exact future Work has occurred. That discards useful architecture and delays the observations that would test it. The practitioner needs a result that distinguishes what can be framed now, what is qualified now, what belongs elsewhere, and exactly what evidence blocks the stronger conclusion.

### HCD.1:3 - Forces

- A short role label is easy to communicate, while the consequential demand usually lives in case variants, interfaces, and error boundaries.
- A MethodDescription can make intended Work inspectable, while only enactment or qualified representative evidence supports claims about obtaining Work and its results.
- Current task success is visible, while later transfer and unfamiliar variants must be established rather than presumed.
- AI and other supports can improve the joint result, while assisted output is not automatically human capability.
- Population summaries help allocation, while a person-specific decision requires a person-specific holder and compatible evidence.
- Missing evidence should stop false certainty, while a useful prospective hypothesis may still guide observation.
- HCD can organize demand evidence, while target-domain correctness, specialist judgement, assignment, safety, release, and employment authority stay elsewhere.

### HCD.1:4 - Solution

Derive demand claim by claim from representative later Work. Preserve the truth posture of every case and source, separate human contributions from surrounding conditions and other owners, and return the strongest conclusion that the evidence supports.

#### HCD.1:4.1 - Pattern-Use Unfolding

1. **Fix the use boundary and holder.** Name the exact human person, receiving decision, later-Work family, subject configuration, effective horizon, and evidence window. If the question concerns a population, identify the separately observed members or representative accounts; do not manufacture one aggregate holder.
2. **Recover the later-Work basis.** For every case, distinguish what was observed from what is planned, stipulated, or assumed. Explain which later Work and material conditions the case represents, and why its evidence is suitable for the intended conclusion. Recover the intended result, quality and critical-error predicates, Work products, and stopping conditions.
3. **Recover Methods and direct-owner inputs.** Identify applicable target Methods or MethodDescriptions, specialist results, assignments, authority, access, environment, tools, and supports. A missing nonhuman value remains a missing nonhuman value; do not rename it a learning gap.
4. **Select representative cases.** Cover the variants that can change demand: familiar and unfamiliar situations, allowed and withdrawn assistance, time or workload limits, AI-output challenge, coordination interfaces, specialist-return conditions, and material quality or critical-error boundaries.
5. **Derive human demand claims.** For each case, state which contribution the named person must be able to make for the intended result under the protected conditions and why the evidence warrants that requirement. Contributions may include, for example, noticing a signal, handling an object, coordinating a specialist return, or justifying a decision. Record required tools and supports as conditions of the contribution. Keep each contribution distinct from the Method, task, tool output, team result, or personal trait.
6. **Bind evidence and uncertainty.** Relate each demand claim to the cases, observations, work products, inference rule, uncertainty, and remaining observation. Keep instruction events, learner events, learning outcomes, later transfer, and job performance distinct; one does not establish the next by label alone.
7. **Return a conclusion-specific disposition.** Emit `qualified current demand`, `prospective demand hypothesis`, `outside-HCD return`, or `demand-evidence-blocked`. Different questions may lawfully receive different dispositions in one use—for example, a prospective hypothesis for future framing and a blocker for qualified current demand.

#### HCD.1:4.2 - Record the Result

A human capability-demand account contains these result positions:

| Result position | Required content |
| --- | --- |
| use boundary | Human holder, receiving decision, later-Work family, configuration, effective horizon, evidence window, and direct owners whose results or authority matter. |
| later-Work basis | Cases, what was observed or remains planned, stipulated, or assumed, and the later Work and conditions they represent; reasons the evidence is suitable for the conclusion; intended result, quality and critical-error predicates, Work products, and stop conditions. |
| protected conditions | Allowed or required tools, AI posture, information access, time, environment, assignment, authority, supports, and specialist interfaces. |
| demand claims | Statements of the human contributions required in the named later Work, including material interfaces among simultaneous contributions. Derive these requirements from the cases rather than assigning capability from job labels or traits. |
| evidence account | Evidence and inference for every demand claim, uncertainty, incompatible evidence, and the observation or case still needed. |
| external gaps | Missing Method, description, specialist return, assignment, authority, access, environment, or nonhuman capability kept under its own kind and owner. |
| disposition | One conclusion-specific `qualified current demand`, `prospective demand hypothesis`, `outside-HCD return`, or `demand-evidence-blocked`. |
| continuation | Compatible inputs that HCD.3, HCD.4, or a future programme comparison may consume, plus scope limits and reopen conditions. |

A `demand-evidence-blocked` result records one row per absent consumed value:

| Missing-value field | Required content |
| --- | --- |
| missing kind | For example `later-Work-occurrence-missing`, `later-Work-result-claim-missing`, `result-record-missing`, `representative-observation-evidence-missing`, or a named external supplier result. |
| blocked conclusion | The exact conclusion that cannot be supported without this value. |
| available non-substitute, when relevant | If available material could plausibly be mistaken for the missing input, name it and explain why it cannot fill this gap. Otherwise omit this field. |
| retry condition | The input that must become available or be confirmed before reconsidering the named conclusion. State its required connection to the person, Work, and conditions, using only qualifiers that affect that conclusion. |

Do not infer `result-record-missing` when qualified direct observations are sufficient for the conclusion. Conversely, an orphan record does not establish that Work occurred or produced the result described in the record.

#### HCD.1:4.3 - Population and Reuse Boundary

A population use may return a distribution over separately identified member or representative demand accounts. It does not create one person's demand by averaging, and it does not turn the population into a capability holder. A population whole qualifies as a holder only through the independent-System condition of `E.23.CDI`, outside this one-human-holder pattern.

Any downstream use must preserve the same human holder, configuration, later-Work conditions, effective horizon, compatible evidence window, and receiving decision. HCD.3 may consume the account as evidence input; HCD.4 may consume it as a scope and result frame. Programme comparison (`HCD.2`) may use the same frame. Deriving demand requires no programme-comparison result.

#### HCD.1:4.4 - What Changes in Practice

A practitioner stops asking, “Which course matches this role?” and first asks, “What must this person contribute in which later Work, under which conditions, and what evidence makes that demand current?” The answer either supports a bounded demand, makes a future hypothesis testable, returns the question to its owner, or exposes the exact missing evidence.

### HCD.1:5 - Archetypal Grounding

#### HCD.1:5.1 - Constructed Positive Case: ImpactReview-17

`ImpactReview-17` is a constructed pedagogical case. Its answer key, `ImpactReview-17-Domain-Oracle-v1`, supplies the engineering correctness and criticality criteria for `PumpWorks-Config-Baseline-42` and `AI-Assist-M4`. The review events and evidence below are stipulated case data.

The human holder is Engineer-P14. The later-Work family is independent review of AI-proposed change-impact claims for mixed physical/software pump-controller changes before separate integration, assurance, and release decisions. The constructed 2026-Q4 conditions include the named configuration and AI assistant, current source access, a forty-five-minute case limit, recoverable audit records, and bounded safety and maintenance specialist returns. Final integration, safety, release, and employment authority remain with their direct owners.

The evidence packet stipulates eight representative review occurrences: four familiar and four unfamiliar variants; four include a plausible but wrong AI dependency claim; two require a specialist return; and every case requires a recoverable audit record. HCD.1 returns this human capability demand for Engineer-P14:

- recognize when the independent-review Method applies;
- trace each release-affecting claim to the current source and configuration;
- independently challenge plausible AI output rather than treating supported output as the person's contribution;
- request, delimit, and preserve required specialist returns;
- return auditable evidence; and
- detect every seeded safety-critical omission within the protected time and authority boundary.

Within this example, the stipulated criteria and observations support `qualified current demand` for Engineer-P14. The case shows how to derive required human contributions from representative Work; demand does not establish the person's current capability. Operational use needs qualified current target-domain criteria, safety or specialist inputs, and representative evidence for the actual person and Work.

#### HCD.1:5.2 - Missing Case: ReleaseWork-R9

For Engineer-E27, the accepted architecture supports a **prospective demand hypothesis** for 2026-Q4 `ReleaseWork-R9`: AI-output challenge, configuration-impact reasoning, evidence return, specialist coordination, critical-error detection, and adaptation across unfamiliar mixed physical/software variants may be required. This hypothesis guides observation; it is not qualified current demand.

The stronger conclusion is blocked by distinct absent values:

| Missing value | Blocked HCD.1 use | Non-substitute and retry |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `XRI-21-supplier-result-missing` | Any demand claim that consumes current engineering-culture enactment, its bounded practice-change decision, retained alternatives, or evidence gaps. | The current `SYSE.21` MethodDescription and its constructed PumpWorks demonstration can shape questions but are not an obtaining supplier result. Retry with a compatible obtaining `SYSE.21` result—or a receiver-permitted qualified direct substitute—for Engineer-E27, the Method, Work, period, and evidence use. |
| `later-Work-occurrence-missing` | Treating ReleaseWork-R9 itself as current later-Work evidence. | A plan, architecture, MethodDescription, or constructed occurrence does not establish enactment. Retry when an admitted performed Work occurrence identifies Engineer-E27, configuration, period, and actual performer. |
| `later-Work-result-claim-missing` | Any demand claim whose evidence consumes the occurrence-produced result, quality, error, or consequence. | A planned result or demonstration result is not the occurrence's result. Retry with the produced result or a supported direct claim tied to the admitted occurrence. |
| `result-record-missing` | Any record-dependent inference about that occurrence or result. | An orphan document would not establish the Work or result. Retry when a current result record links the admitted Work occurrence to its produced result; omit this requirement where qualified direct observations are sufficient. |
| `representative-observation-evidence-missing` | Upgrading the prospective hypothesis to `qualified current demand`. | A target repertoire, curriculum, course completion, platform score, or constructed case is not representative observation evidence. Retry with holder- and condition-compatible observations or a named qualified representative case set covering performed Method, configuration, work products, quality and critical errors, AI/support posture, specialist returns, variants, and evidence window. |

These rows do not collapse into “insufficient data.” Each names a different missing input, blocked conclusion, misleading substitute, and retry condition. Recovering one row changes only the conclusions that consume it.

#### HCD.1:5.3 - Unlike Case: Clinical Ownership

Return an adult's request for treatment of social anxiety to a qualified clinical specialist as an `outside-HCD return`. HCD.1 may address a separately posed, legitimate question about that person's non-clinical participation in later Work, supplied by the person or qualified clinical owner. Diagnosis, treatment choice, provider competence, safety, consent, and clinical outcome interpretation remain with the qualified clinical route.

### HCD.1:6 - Bias-Annotation

| Recurring bias | Likely drift | Repair |
| --- | --- | --- |
| training-solution bias | The requested course or certification is treated as the demand. | Recover the human holder and representative later Work before considering any learning product. |
| role-average bias | A job-family profile is assigned to one person or one population average is reified as a person's demand. | Keep holder-specific accounts and report population distributions separately. |
| current-arrangement bias | Today's Method, tool, or division of labour is assumed to be the only future demand. | Name the effective horizon, alternatives, supports, and configuration; preserve prospective uncertainty. |
| technology bias | AI-assisted or tool-supported output is attributed to the human holder. | Separate human, tool, specialist, team, and organization contributions and test material support conditions. |
| evidence-convenience bias | Completion, self-report, platform prediction, MethodDescription, or visible document substitutes for later-Work evidence. | Trace each demand claim to representative cases, observations, inference, uncertainty, and remaining evidence. |
| human-deficit bias | Missing access, authority, assignment, Method, environment, specialist result, or nonhuman capability is called a skill gap. | Return the value under its actual kind and direct owner. |
| occurrence maximalism | No future question is allowed until the exact Work occurs. | Return a prospective demand hypothesis with observations-to-obtain, while blocking only the stronger current conclusion. |

### HCD.1:7 - Conformance Checklist

- [ ] One exact human person is named as holder; any population use preserves member or representative accounts.
- [ ] The later-Work family, configuration, effective horizon, evidence window, and receiving decision are explicit.
- [ ] Each case distinguishes observed facts from plans, stipulations, and assumptions, and explains which later-Work conditions it represents and why its evidence is suitable for the conclusion.
- [ ] Intended results, quality and critical-error predicates, protected conditions, tools, supports, and specialist interfaces are recoverable.
- [ ] Each demand claim names a human contribution supported by representative Work rather than a job label, trait, task, MethodDescription, or course.
- [ ] Human, AI, tool, specialist, team, organization, assignment, authority, and environment contributions or gaps remain distinct.
- [ ] Each demand claim has evidence, an inference boundary, uncertainty, and any observation still needed.
- [ ] The result uses one conclusion-specific disposition and does not suppress a lawful prospective hypothesis merely because qualified current demand is blocked.
- [ ] Every blocker names the missing input, blocked conclusion, and retry condition; available material is distinguished from that input when it could mislead the receiver.
- [ ] Downstream reuse preserves holder, configuration, conditions, horizon, evidence window, and receiving decision.
- [ ] The first result selects no curriculum, provider, intervention, assessment programme, treatment, safety action, release action, or employment action.


### HCD.1:8 - Common Anti-Patterns and How to Avoid Them

| Anti-pattern | Why it fails | Repair |
| --- | --- | --- |
| “The role requires these competencies” | The role label hides case variants, conditions, and the exact holder. | Derive claim-sized contributions from representative later Work for the named person. |
| “The course objective is the capability demand” | A learning-product intention is not a later-Work result. | Start with later Work and let a future programme comparison answer the demand. |
| “The MethodDescription proves the Work” | A description states an intended Method; it does not establish enactment, result, or observation. | Preserve its use as a question frame and recover the missing occurrence or qualified representative evidence. |
| “The tool score is the person's capability” | The score may describe joint or model-dependent performance. | State support conditions and recover direct task evidence and inference limits. |
| “One missing record means no evidence exists” | Direct observations may support the conclusion without a record, while an orphan record may support nothing about the occurrence. | Name the evidence actually consumed and require a record only for a record-dependent use. |
| “The average employee is the holder” | Aggregation erases person, condition, and evidence identity. | Keep individual accounts or an explicit distribution; use `E.23.CDI` before treating a population whole as holder. |
| “HCD.2 must come next” | Numbering is mistaken for a lifecycle and invites premature programme design. | Route independently to HCD.3 or HCD.4 when their qualified inputs exist; programme comparison needs its own qualified basis and decision. |
| “Poor outcome means training” | Method, tool, assignment, access, organization, nonhuman capability, or specialist causes may dominate. | Return those values to their owners and use HCD.3 only when a diagnostic question remains. |

### HCD.1:9 - Consequences

The organization gains an inspectable demand account that can support diagnosis, profile comparison, evidence collection, or later programme comparison without making those later decisions in advance. Prospective planning remains possible, and missing evidence becomes a substantive return rather than an administrative “TBD.”

The cost is more careful case selection and relation keeping. Demand may vary across configurations, supports, horizons, and holders; some visible requests will return outside HCD. Evidence collection may reveal that the main constraint is not human capability.

### HCD.1:10 - Rationale

Beginning from representative later Work preserves the direction of justification: intended results and conditions constrain the required human contributions; learning products do not invent their own demand. Holder identity prevents a role model or cohort aggregate from masquerading as a person's capability.

The four dispositions avoid a false choice between unsupported certainty and silence. A prospective hypothesis is useful for future framing, while a discriminated blocker protects a stronger current conclusion. Keeping external gaps under their own kinds prevents HCD from taking authority over target Methods, specialist results, assignments, safety, release, or employment.

### HCD.1:11 - SoTA-Echoing

The working question is: **How should a practitioner derive a human capability demand that is useful for later development decisions and still warranted by later-Work evidence?**

The selected current line combines representative-work analysis with the workplace-instruction evidence chain summarized by Kraiger and Ford's 2021 review ([`COG-10`](https://doi.org/10.1146/annurev-orgpsych-012420-060109)): instructional events, learner events, learning outcomes, transfer, and job performance must be distinguished, and claims about each need corresponding evidence. Transfer must be established under material learner, design, opportunity, support, and work-environment conditions. Direct target-domain evidence supplies what counts as correct, safe, critical, or useful in the actual Work. Where AI assistance is material, Dell’Acqua and colleagues’ [Navigating the jagged technological frontier](https://doi.org/10.1287/orsc.2025.21838) (`AI-03`) supplies a bounded cue to test independent error detection under the named task and model conditions. It establishes neither a universal AI-task boundary nor this person's engineering capability.

A serious operational alternative is a role-first competency framework or training-needs inventory. It is cheaper to publish and supports broad workforce communication, but it commonly loses holder identity, representative case variation, tool and specialist interfaces, critical-error predicates, and the distinction between a learning outcome and later performance. HCD.1 retains those inventories only as discovery cues and mutates them into evidence-bound claim rows.

The selected line does not establish one universal demand, transfer trajectory, or intervention for ReleaseWork-R9. It also cannot supply target-domain truth. Reopen a demand claim when its holder, Work family, Method, configuration, support posture, quality or critical-error predicate, effective horizon, evidence window, direct source, or receiving decision changes. A later stronger source reopens only the claims whose move or limit it changes.

### HCD.1:12 - Relations

- **`A.15.1` and current Work ontology:** keep Method, Work occurrence, performer, result, and result episteme distinct.
- **`E.23.CDI`:** governs whether a population whole is an independent System capable of holding capability; HCD.1 otherwise stays at one-human-holder granularity.
- **`E.23.CAE`:** supplies capability-evidence boundaries for downstream observations; evidence does not itself authorize intervention or employment action.
- **`COG-10`:** supplies the event-to-transfer distinction and the requirement to qualify workplace opportunity, support, and environment.
- **[Dell’Acqua and colleagues, Navigating the jagged technological frontier](https://doi.org/10.1287/orsc.2025.21838) (`AI-03`):** supplies the bounded independent-error-challenge cue where assisted Work makes that contribution material.
- **Target-domain Methods and results:** determine correctness, quality, criticality, and specialist boundaries. HCD.1 consumes them; it does not replace them.
- **`SYSE.21` / `XRI-21`:** an obtaining compatible cultural-continuation result may supply later-enactment observations, a bounded practice-change decision, retained alternatives, and evidence gaps. Its MethodDescription or constructed demonstration is not that supplier result.
- **`HCD.3`:** may consume a compatible HCD.1 account as evidence input for a limiting-capability or non-training disposition; no target is entailed.
- **`HCD.4`:** may consume a compatible HCD.1 account as the scope and result frame for a condition-qualified profile comparison; no mastery or choice is entailed.
- **Programme comparison (`HCD.2`):** may consume the compatible demand account and evidence needs. HCD.1 derives demand without selecting a programme or requiring a programme-comparison result.
- **Clinical, safety, legal, employment, specialist, organization, provider, and release owners:** retain their direct decisions and authority.

### HCD.1:End

<a id="hcd-3"></a>
## HCD.3 — Diagnose the Limiting Capability, Misconception, or Behaviour

> **Type:** Human Capability Development practitioner diagnostic pattern


### HCD.3:0 - Use This When

Use this pattern when a named person's later-Work demand is already recoverable, performance is inadequate or unstable, and different explanations would lead to different next Work. Begin by comparing the smallest rival set that could change the decision; do not begin by assigning training, practice, coaching, motivation work, or treatment.

The first useful result is either a **qualified intervention target**—the smallest changeable human limitation supported by current evidence—or a **non-training return** to the owner of the actual constraint, specialist question, or missing result. A target names what is supported, any still-plausible rival explanations, the conditions protected during observation, and what later observation could reopen it. A return names the blocked HCD decision and an exact retry condition.

The practical gain is stopping generic development Work before it consumes time or harms the person. Use `HCD.1` when the later-Work demand itself is unclear. Use `HCD.4` after a compatible demand and claim-level evidence exist and simultaneous capability claims must be compared. A separate programme comparison (`HCD.2`) may use a target or another qualified basis; HCD.3 stops at the target or non-training return.

Do not use this pattern to diagnose a medical disorder, prescribe a clinical or learning intervention, declare that development occurred, authorize surveillance, assign Work, or make safety, employment, provider, or treatment decisions.

### HCD.3:0.1 - Working Terms

| Name in this pattern | What it denotes |
| --- | --- |
| diagnostic question | A bounded question about which live explanation best accounts for an observed later-Work gap and would change the next Work. It is not a medical diagnosis. |
| rival branch | A materially different explanation whose survival changes the next observation, owner, or development question. |
| human limitation | A difficulty this person has in making a required contribution under the named later-Work conditions. Current evidence supports treating that difficulty as changeable and relevant to the decision. |
| qualified intervention target | The smallest supported human limitation, with evidence, any still-plausible rivals, conditions, intended observation, specialist boundary, and reopen condition. “Intervention target” names what a later choice might try to change; it selects no intervention. |
| non-training return | A return to the owner of a nonhuman constraint, specialist question, or question outside the demand envelope; or a return naming evidence needed before a human target can be justified. |
| protected condition | A condition held stable or bounded so that an observation can discriminate rivals without quietly changing the holder, task, tool, authority, or support arrangement. |
| target engagement | A change in the selected target attributable to an intervention. Evidence must concern that target and justify the attribution; a theory label or assigned activity does neither. |

The diagnosis concerns the person's difficulty making the required contribution under the named conditions. A low score, poor result, behaviour label, or inferred latent state can inform the diagnosis but does not by itself show what human change would help.

### HCD.3:1 - Problem Frame

When Work goes badly, the visible human is often the easiest place to intervene. Yet the same observation can arise because the demand was wrong, the Method or source was missing, a tool or cue changed, the assignment or authority failed, workload or recovery was inadequate, a specialist return was absent, the response was not recognized as applicable, or the person's capability really is limiting.

These branches have different owners and remedies. More practice can be useless when the task lies outside the claimed envelope, unsafe when a clinical or safety condition is current, and misleading when assistance or prompt design controls the result. Conversely, an endless list of possible causes is not a diagnosis. The comparison must become small enough to change a decision.

### HCD.3:2 - Problem

The common shortcut is to transform “the result was poor” into “the person needs training.” It hides the evidence chain, turns a familiar intervention into a cause, and exposes the person to monitoring or employment consequences without a justified target.

The opposite shortcut refuses any bounded target while uncertainty remains. That makes practical diagnosis impossible even when controlled observations have eliminated the decision-changing rivals. The practitioner needs a conservative result that can name one qualified target, record the explanations considered and any still-plausible alternatives, and return questions for other owners. Eliminating the considered rivals leaves the stated evidence limits and reopen conditions in force.

### HCD.3:3 - Forces

- A familiar human explanation is actionable, while Method, tool, authority, organization, environment, or specialist constraints may be more decisive.
- One observation is cheap, while a target must survive the rivals that would change the next Work.
- A controlled probe improves discrimination, while the probe can teach, prime, fatigue, expose, or otherwise change the person.
- Assisted performance reveals a joint result, while the human limitation may concern applicability, access, adaptation, enactment, or independent challenge.
- Motivation language is easy to apply, while change in the proposed target needs evidence beyond a theory label or assigned technique.
- A narrow target supports later design, while a diagnosis that is too broad becomes a trait label or a curriculum in disguise.
- Useful evidence can be collected, while privacy, consent, worker governance, clinical safety, and employment authority constrain collection and use.
- A specialist boundary protects the person, while HCD must still preserve any legitimate non-clinical Work question.

### HCD.3:4 - Solution

Compare the coarsest decision-changing rival branches, run only safe and qualified observations, and return the smallest human limitation justified by the current evidence—or return the question to the owner of the surviving non-training branch.

#### HCD.3:4.1 - Bind the Diagnostic Use

Name:

1. the exact human holder and participating basis;
2. the HCD.1 demand account or equivalent qualified later-Work input, including configuration, conditions, horizon, and evidence window;
3. the observed gap and the HCD decision it could change;
4. the evidence collector, declared use, access conditions, privacy or worker-governance boundary, and direct owners;
5. the protected conditions and safe probe limit; and
6. the point at which a clinical, safety, legal, employment, target-domain, or other specialist owner must take over.

If the demand is absent or incompatible, stop with a non-training return for the exact missing scope or evidence. Do not diagnose a person against a job label, aggregate profile, or imagined future task.

#### HCD.3:4.2 - Compare Decision-Changing Rivals

Include at least the branches that remain plausible and would change the next Work:

| Rival branch | Question that keeps it live |
| --- | --- |
| demand or envelope | Is the demand specified correctly and inside the capability envelope being relied upon? |
| applicability, access, expression, adaptation, or enactment | Is the response unavailable, or does a cue, context, interference, configuration, or performer arrangement prevent selection or use? |
| Method or description | Is the target Method missing, stale, disputed, or inapplicable? |
| tools and supports | Are AI, information, cues, access, environment, workload, recovery, or another support condition limiting? |
| assignment and organization | Is assignment, permission, authority, coordination, organization, provider access, or a required specialist result missing? |
| specialist condition | Does health, disorder, safety, ethics, law, or another specialist condition require its direct owner? |
| human change target | Does the evidence support a particular capability, knowledge, skill, discrimination, misconception, behaviour, self-regulation, or commitment as limiting and changeable? |
| measurement artefact | Could a scoring, recording, or interpretation error explain the poor indicator without the corresponding failure in Work? |

Do not expand the set with every imaginable explanation. Include a branch when a credible current value or absence makes it decision-changing.

Too few or poorly linked observations, coupled conditions, contamination, or unsafe collection can prevent this comparison from distinguishing the live branches. Narrow the conclusion or give a non-training return that names the evidence limit, the next needed observation, and safe conditions for obtaining it.

#### HCD.3:4.3 - Probe Before Declaring Capability Loss

When a prior qualified capability or result exists and the differential can change the next Work, use an applicable `E.23.CAE` disposition before declaring loss or prescribing more practice. Return to a qualified reference condition without further development where safe, vary the smallest decision-bearing condition, and observe applicability, access, adaptation, enactment, and result separately.

A recovered response weakens simple global loss but does not prove transfer. Failure across reference and target conditions may warrant capability-claim revision only after demand, configuration, support, and measurement rivals have been addressed.
