| “The tool changed, so this is a new Method variant.” | Compare reusable semantics and return description, support, tool, or local-Work changes to their direct owner. |
| “The candidate worked locally, so adopt it.” | Preserve candidate status and return Method admission, fit and worth to ME; HCD decides only its bounded evidence use. |
| “Assisted scores improved, so learning occurred.” | Obtain unassisted, changed-support, transfer, retention, or other direct evidence required by the claim. |
| “One subgroup failed, so retire the Method everywhere.” | Branch or retire only the defeated population, condition, or use and preserve supported uses. |
| “Refresh the whole publication after every source change.” | Reopen the affected claim; use G.11 only for a maintained currentness object under its own conditions. |

### HCD.16:9 - Consequences

The HCD repertoire becomes smaller, more explicit, and easier to repair. Practitioners can identify which Method use remains warranted, which claim changed, which branch needs direct evidence, and which candidate must stay a candidate. Source or tool changes no longer force a global rebuild.

The cost is disciplined evidence binding. Some decisions stop at `further probe`, and a useful local Method can be retired from one use while remaining available elsewhere. Method Engineering, providers, target-domain owners, and evidence owners may need to supply separate results before HCD can act.

### HCD.16:10 - Rationale

The remaining HCD question is not whether a Method exists in general. It is whether an exact human-development claim remains warranted for a bounded population, later Work, provider and support arrangement, evidence window, and receiving use.

Separating this question from Method admission and generic repertoire maintenance preserves reusable semantics and lineage while letting direct human-development evidence change practice. Claim-sized dispositions avoid both fashion-driven replacement and loyalty-driven stagnation.

### HCD.16:11 - SoTA-Echoing

**Working question:** how should a human-capability-development repertoire change when sources, tools, learner conditions, or direct evidence disagree?

**Selected line — adopt and adapt.** The best-known defensible line for this bounded question is claim-sized updating that preserves population, task, comparator, measure, horizon, support, provider, and Method status. Brynjolfsson, Li, and Raymond's [deployed customer-support study](https://doi.org/10.1093/qje/qjae044) separates assisted productivity from a noisy same-job outage signal; Dell'Acqua and colleagues' [task-frontier experiment](https://doi.org/10.1287/orsc.2025.21838) supplies a bounded independent-error challenge; and Bastani and colleagues' [school-mathematics trial](https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.2422633122) separates assisted practice from later unassisted performance. HCD.16 adapts these unlike evidence states in §§4.3–4.6 and §5.2 rather than pooling them into one AI-learning verdict. None establishes E27's capability, a universal tutor, other-Work transfer, or long retention.

The serious default is repertoire change by newest source, popular school, average score, or tool release. It is cheap, but it loses claim scope and makes a source identity or visible proxy decide what only receiving-use evidence can decide. HCD.16 rejects that default and accepts the extra work of binding evidence because the result is better on critical-error safety, transfer truth, status preservation, and local repair while remaining actionable through five bounded dispositions.

Bauer and colleagues' [review of counterconditioning and reward-related extinction directions](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.nlm.2024.107994) and the Mertens/Maier [choice-architecture synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.2107346118) and [publication-bias critique](https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.2200300119) serve as failure and contradiction evidence: a credible current line may be emerging, heterogeneous, or sensitive to bias without supporting a universal HCD or specialist intervention. This changes the source-row and `further probe` rules in §§4.4–4.6 and the domain return in §5.3.

Current ME.15 and ME.16 supply the stronger internal alternative for generic Method semantics, lineage, introduction, observations, and maintained returns. HCD.16 consumes those results and rejects duplicating them. Reopen the comparison when stronger direct evidence changes an affected population, Work, learner state, provider, tool, support, measure, horizon, causal limit, or source role, or when an improved Method-repertoire practice returns the same status honesty and local repair at lower decision effort.

### HCD.16:12 - Relations

Observed HCD.15 arrangement use and human-development evidence may supply a compatible HCD.16 input. A designed arrangement, attendance record, community membership, or provider activity does not establish Method fit, transfer, retention, or worth. Equivalent direct evidence permits independent entry.

ME.15 supplies admitted Method semantics or a status-preserved candidate lineage, provenance, applicability, evidence positions, currentness, and retirement meaning. ME.16 may supply introduction and later-use observations while keeping target, strategy, description, plan, Work, capability, authority, and outside results distinct. HCD.16 returns only the exact contradicted generic claim, edition, support result, or next action to its ME owner.

HCD.1–HCD.14 may supply qualified demand, target, programme, task, support, practice, performance, transfer, retention, and revision evidence. HCD.16 neither reconstructs nor upgrades those results.

A bounded HCD.16 disposition or status-preserved variation account may supply HCD.17 with one cultural-variation input. It does not establish actual enactment, receiving culture, population prevalence, or intervention authority.

G.5 declares a selector-facing shortlist, ranked shortlist, joint-use set, handoff, abstain, or escalation only after exact members and basis exist. G.11 scopes currentness refresh for a named maintained object. C.11 owns an actual local choice. Clinical, safety, legal, employment, provider, organization, platform, learning-product, and target-domain owners retain their Methods, correctness, service, and authority.

### HCD.16:End

<a id="hcd-17"></a>
## HCD.17 — Deliberately Continue and Change Human Capability-Development Culture

> **Type:** Human Capability Development practitioner method pattern
> **Status:** Current practitioner preview

### HCD.17:1 - Problem Frame

**Use this when** a bounded provider, learner, practitioner, or mixed Human Capability Development population intends to continue or change one observable way in which HCD practice is generated, transmitted, enacted by receivers, recognized, selected, remembered, retained, or lost.

Begin with the HCD Discipline or a smaller named practice, the population, place and period, a truthful admitted-Method or candidate/observed-practice branch, and one testable cultural predicate. The first useful result is a current enacted-culture account or an honest candidate/practice account, one bounded `continue`, `revise`, `branch`, `replace`, `stop`, or `unknown` decision, later observations, retained alternatives, and explicit evidence gaps.

What this buys in practice is a change small enough to observe. Providers and practitioners can improve one transmission, receiving-use, recognition, selection, memory, retention, generation, or loss relation without claiming field-wide adoption, permanent culture, or improved capability from a carrier or label.

Do not use HCD.17 for one person's capability, one Method introduction, a learning product's curriculum or carrier choice, generic cultural-evolution modeling, Method-Engineering culture, organization-holder working culture or change, or a publication and standard history alone. Return those questions to HCD.11–HCD.16, C.36, ME.17, OCE, the Learning Product owner, or the direct publication and institutional owner.

### HCD.17:2 - Problem

Human Capability Development is carried through teaching and practice Work, providers, peers, assignments, feedback, assessments, communities, descriptions, tools, repositories, and remembered cases. Those visible subjects make it tempting to say that a school, syllabus, standard, platform, rollout, value statement, or published edition “is the culture.”

The label hides the relation. A document may have been generated and published without being received. A practice may be taught without being selected, recognized, or retained. A mandatory field may be completed without actual development Work. A community may be active without changing the human contribution later relied on.

Deliberate change then becomes ceremonial: publish a new carrier, run an event, or announce adoption and infer that the culture and capability changed. HCD.17 instead makes one cultural predicate, population, authority, competing explanation, intervention, later observation, and human-development consequence separately inspectable.

### HCD.17:3 - Forces

| Force | Working tension |
| --- | --- |
| Familiar labels and testable relations | Schools, standards and communities aid recognition, while the label may conceal generation, transmission, receiving use, selection, memory, retention or loss. |
| Carriers and practice | Descriptions, tools and forums can participate in continuation, while their presence does not establish actual HCD Work or culture. |
| Method status and observation | An admitted Method can support an enactment claim, while candidate and source-described practice must remain usable without status laundering. |
| Local action and population scope | One authorized provider slice can change its arrangement, while it rarely controls a field or every participant. |
| Culture and human capability | A cultural relation may matter because later capability matters, while neither observation proves the other or their causal link. |
| Timely learning and retention | Receiving use can be observed soon, while long-term retention and loss require later intervals. |
| Intervention and explanation | A bounded reversible change is useful, while at least two serious hypotheses must survive until a discriminating observation. |

### HCD.17:4 - Solution

#### HCD.17:4.1 - Bound the HCD practice, population, and decision

Name the HCD Discipline when a current direct result recognizes it, or name the smaller practice with the Discipline claim unresolved. Identify provider, learner, practitioner, transmitting, receiving, recognizing, selecting, remembering, retaining, generating, or losing participants as the predicate requires. State the place, period, later-Work family, qualification window, and bounded cultural-continuation decision.

Avoid “the organization,” “the profession,” or “the community” without participant meanings and a population boundary. A local provider group and its assigned learners can be sufficient. Wider field or organization claims need their own population and evidence.

If the population, period, practice, or decision cannot be bounded, return the exact missing value. Do not compensate with an adoption score.

#### HCD.17:4.2 - Choose the truthful Method or candidate branch

Use the **admitted-Method branch** only when one independently admitted HCD Method or variant and actual HCD Work with a grounded enactment relation are recoverable for the current claim. Teaching a MethodDescription, publishing a card, or configuring a tool is not enactment.

Use the **candidate or observed-practice branch** when the subject is a status-preserved candidate, source-described practice, local routine, card, template, description episteme, or other claim-bearing material. Record independently supported Work facts without calling the candidate a Method, the material a `MethodDescription`, or the Work an enactment of the candidate whole.

Preserve the branch across the result. If admission, description membership, reusable semantics, lineage, or introduction becomes the live question, return it to Method Engineering. HCD.17 governs the HCD cultural relation, not the generic status repair.

#### HCD.17:4.3 - State one cultural value and predicate

Name the exact value said to be generated, transmitted, received in practice, recognized, selected, remembered, retained, or lost. Then state:

- the transmitting, receiving, recognizing, selecting, remembering, retaining, generating, or losing participant meanings;
- applicability conditions and qualification window;
- a positive case;
- a discriminating negative, failed, inapplicable, unknown, or missing-information case; and
- the decision that a changed observation can affect.

Use an existing direct predicate when it fits. Otherwise retain the lightest truthful bounded relation-bearing claim through the current FPF relation method. A convenient cultural label does not create a relation kind or occurrence.

Record other cultural claims separately. Generation, publication, transmission, receiving use, recognition, selection, memory, retention, and loss can differ in result; do not average them into adoption or maturity.

#### HCD.17:4.4 - Identify the actual carriers, Work, and participating subjects

Name only subjects and direct relations used by the claim: practice cases, feedback and assessment records, MethodDescriptions or candidate materials with status, publication occurrences, repositories, tools, AI configurations, community or forum Systems, teaching or tutoring Work, provider and learner Work, decision rules, assignments, permissions, authority, access, support, and memory epistemes.

For every claimed actual Work occurrence, identify the performer and apply the direct Work and Method tests. A bundle word such as “programme,” “rollout,” “platform,” or “school” establishes none of its implied subjects or relations.

Select a structure or architecture only when its organization changes the cultural decision. Presence in the carrier list does not prove transmission, receiving use, selection, retention, or capability.

#### HCD.17:4.5 - Recover intervention authority and protected conditions

Name the change Agent and the exact assignment, permission, or authority predicate, participants, permitted action, scope, positive basis, and protected conditions. Ownership, expertise, sponsorship, project position, assignment, permission, and authority are different.

Return `missing-change-authorization` when the proposed intervention exceeds the recovered scope. Keep organization change, employment, clinical care, provider service, platform administration, learning-product design, privacy, safety, and target-domain authority with their direct owners.

State participation, consent, privacy, access, workload, exclusion, accessibility, service-pressure, and restoration conditions that matter. Stop or revert when the intervention breaches them.

#### HCD.17:4.6 - Keep human capability and later Work as a separate consequence

Name the intended human capability and later-Work consequence, baseline, observation, evidence window, and uncertainty separately from the cultural predicate. Use HCD.11, HCD.12, HCD.13, or qualified equivalents for performance, unfamiliar transfer, delayed performance, and support dependence.

A positive cultural predicate can coexist with no demonstrated capability change. A favourable capability observation can coexist with an unknown transmission or selection relation. If the decision relies on the cultural intervention causing the consequence, obtain a suitable causal result; timing or co-occurrence is insufficient.

#### HCD.17:4.7 - Compare hypotheses and select one informative intervention

Keep at least two serious non-equivalent hypotheses about how the predicate obtains or fails. Examples include carrier sufficiency versus coached feedback, formal selection versus local workaround, community participation versus supervisor access, or remembered description versus repeated receiving Work.

For each hypothesis, state an observation that would be more likely if it were true. Compare retaining the current arrangement, changing one Method/support/assessment/carrier relation, branching for a bounded population, replacing the means, and stopping or reverting where material.

Select one authorized, reversible, proportionate intervention and one later observation that can discriminate the live hypotheses. State burden, protected conditions, negative case, and response rule. Return `unknown` when the observation cannot discriminate; do not force a positive cultural verdict.

#### HCD.17:4.8 - Observe later claims and return the bounded decision

At the named later interval, apply the same predicate to the stated participants or an explicitly changed population. Record positive, negative, inapplicable, unknown, or missing-information without assuming that a repeated label identifies the same relation occurrence.

Return:

| Position | Required content |
| --- | --- |
| Boundary | HCD Discipline or smaller practice, population and participant meanings, place, period, later Work, qualification window and decision. |
| Subject branch | Admitted Method and grounded actual enactment, or candidate/observed practice with honest materials and Work facts. |
| Cultural claim | One value and predicate, applicability, positive and discriminating negative cases, evidence, intervals and gaps. |
| Participating subjects | Actual carriers, descriptions or candidate materials, publications, Systems, Work, assignments, permissions, authority, access and support relations relied on. |
| Intervention | Change Agent and authority, two serious hypotheses, alternatives, authorized reversible change, burden, protection, and discriminating observation. |
| Separate consequence | Intended human capability and later-Work consequence, HCD evidence, uncertainty and causal non-use. |
| Decision | `continue`, `revise`, `branch`, `replace`, `stop`, or `unknown`; retained alternatives, population and authority limits, return owners and reopen conditions. |

Return the exact missing Method status, Work, predicate, participant, evidence, authority, provider, specialist, or later-observation result when the decision is blocked. A supported cultural claim does not fill another missing claim by proximity.

### HCD.17:5 - Archetypal Grounding

#### HCD.17:5.1 - A constructed E27 cultural-continuation branch

This example is constructed. It supplies a complete decision form but no evidence that `CapabilityDevelopmentTeam-E27`, Engineer-E27, any provider Work, or ReleaseWork-R9 occurred. It preserves every current ReleaseWork-R9 evidence gap.

Bound the practice to the named provider group and assigned release-review practitioners in 2026-Q4. The later-Work family is independent review of AI-assisted mixed physical/software release claims. The intended human consequence is independent generation and testing of a configuration counter-hypothesis before accepting plausible wrong AI output, while preserving source trace, critical-error detection, justified action or refusal, evidence return, and specialist boundaries.

Use the candidate/observed-practice branch for `Counter-Hypothesis-First Candidate-E27`; the example does not admit it as a Method or claim enactment. Stipulate only for the replay that a provider slice has used a scenario card, paired practice case, feedback record, current source set, permitted AI configuration, and coaching session in its development Work.

The primary cultural value is **receiving use before a cue supplies the alternative**. The constructed predicate is positive for one receiving practitioner only when, in a later fresh development-practice occurrence within the named period, that practitioner states and tests a relevant counter-hypothesis before a coach, card prompt, or AI output names it, while preserving trace and specialist return. A discriminating negative is completed fresh practice in which the alternative appears only after such help. Card access, attendance, corrected output, and community discussion do not satisfy the predicate.

Keep participating subjects separate: the candidate account, scenario card, paired cases, feedback records, source set, AI configuration, provider and practitioner Work, coaching Work, review forum, assignments, permissions, specialist access, and later observation. The current replay stipulates authority only for one provider steward to branch the practice arrangement for this provider slice; it grants no authority over ReleaseWork-R9, employment, safety, the wider organization, or the field.

Two hypotheses remain live:

| Hypothesis | Discriminating expectation |
| --- | --- |
| `H-E27-CARRIER` — the revised scenario card and paired cases are sufficient for receiving use | After self-study and carrier access but before coaching, the practitioner applies the counter-hypothesis move on a fresh case. |
| `H-E27-COACH` — criterion-bearing coached feedback supplies the decisive discrimination | Pre-coaching fresh cases remain cue-dependent, while equivalent post-coaching cases show independent first use. |

Return a bounded `branch` decision: preserve the current provider arrangement, add the revised candidate materials only for one authorized slice, and observe one equivalent fresh uncued case before and after coaching while recording help, critical errors, trace, specialist return, burden, and participant conditions. Stop on missing criteria, unsafe practice, absent specialist access, excessive burden, or damage to a protected contribution.

The later result remains conditional:

| Observation | Cultural decision |
| --- | --- |
| Positive cases occur before coaching across the bounded slice | Continue the branch provisionally and weaken the coaching-dependence hypothesis; long-term retention and wider selection remain open. |
| Positive cases occur only after coaching | Revise the carrier-sufficiency claim and retain the combined-support branch; do not call the card alone culturally transmitted. |
| Corrected output occurs but the predicate remains negative | Preserve any practice benefit separately; receiving use remains unproved. |
| Burden rises or trace/specialist return deteriorates | Stop, revert, or redesign the branch even if the target move appears more often. |
| No authorized later observation is available | Return `unknown` with the missing observation and authority; publication or attendance cannot fill it. |

Any later capability claim requires its own HCD.11–HCD.13-compatible evidence. Any causal claim that the branch produced capability change requires a separately justified causal use.

#### HCD.17:5.2 - CDIO edition history is not enacted HCD culture

CDIO version history can support bounded claims about formal generation, edition, optional-standard introduction, institutional selection, and publication. For one engineering programme, an HCD.17 enacted-culture claim still needs the provider and receiving population, exact admitted Method and actual HCD Work or an honest candidate branch, one predicate, carriers, authority, place and period, later observation, human-capability consequence, and gaps.

When those values are absent, return the supported edition or institutional history and the missing enacted-culture evidence. Do not infer “CDIO culture,” prevalence, retention, capability, or effectiveness from the standard or curriculum label.

#### HCD.17:5.3 - Community activity is one contribution, not the cultural result

An online community can supply access, experience exchange, facilitation, coordination and professional support. Those contributions may participate in a transmission or recognition hypothesis. Membership, posts, platform availability, or a favourable survey do not establish the cultural predicate, individual capability, long-term retention, or causal effect. Observe the named receiving or later-practice relation directly.

### HCD.17:6 - Bias-Annotation

Institutional prestige and visible carriers make formal editions, standards, schools, repositories, and events easy proxies for culture. Recover the exact value, participants, predicate, positive and negative cases, and later observation before relying on the label.

Success in one provider slice creates a scale bias: local receiving use is retold as organizational or field adoption. Preserve the population, period, authority, and unobserved links. Conversely, absence of a field-wide study does not block an honestly bounded local probe.

### HCD.17:7 - Conformance Checklist

- The HCD Discipline or smaller practice, bounded population and participant meanings, place, period, later Work, qualification window, and decision are explicit.
- The subject branch is truthful: admitted Method plus grounded actual enactment, or candidate/observed practice without Method, MethodDescription, variant, or enactment inflation.
- One cultural value and predicate has applicability, a positive case, a discriminating negative or failed case, evidence, interval, and gaps.
- Generation, transmission, receiving use, recognition, selection, memory, retention, and loss are separate claims rather than one adoption score.
- Actual carriers, materials with status, publications, Systems, Work, assignments, permissions, authority, access, provider and support relations are named only when relied on.
- The change Agent and exact authority, scope, positive basis, protection, burden, reversibility, stop, and restoration conditions are explicit.
- At least two serious hypotheses, their different predictions, one informative intervention, and one decision-changing later observation are present.
- Human capability and later-Work consequence use their own evidence and remain separate from the cultural predicate and any causal claim.
- The result is `continue`, `revise`, `branch`, `replace`, `stop`, or `unknown`, with retained alternatives, population and authority limits, exact returns and reopen conditions.
- Organization, Method Engineering, learning-product, clinical, employment, safety, platform, provider, publication, and target-domain questions retain their owners.

### HCD.17:8 - Common Anti-Patterns and How to Avoid Them

| Anti-pattern | Better move |
| --- | --- |
| “The standard was adopted, so the culture changed.” | Separate institutional selection and publication from receiving HCD Work, the cultural predicate, later observation, and consequence. |
| “Everyone attended the rollout.” | Treat attendance as one Work or evidence fact; test the named receiving, selection, recognition, memory, retention, or loss predicate. |
| “The cards were used, so the candidate Method was enacted.” | Preserve candidate status and independently establish Method admission and actual enactment before using that branch. |
| “Capability improved, so transmission occurred.” | Assess human capability and the cultural predicate separately; open causal evaluation only when the decision relies on the link. |
| “One team result proves field adoption.” | Keep population, place, period, predicate, authority, and wider gaps visible. |
| “Change the carrier and see whether activity rises.” | Keep at least two hypotheses and choose an observation that discriminates them rather than counting activity. |

### HCD.17:9 - Consequences

Providers and practitioners gain a bounded way to continue or change HCD culture without making the word “culture” carry Method status, Work, publication, authority, capability, and effect at once. Local interventions become reversible and informative; negative, inapplicable, unknown, and missing-information cases remain usable.

The cost is narrower claims and later observation. A visible standard or community may support only one institutional or carrier fact. Long-term retention and loss require time, and a useful human-capability result may still leave the cultural explanation uncertain.

### HCD.17:10 - Rationale

Culture becomes actionable when one value moves through a testable relation among bounded participants and when a later observation can change a decision. HCD.17 adds the HCD-specific provider, learner, practitioner, development-Work, human-capability, and later-Work positions needed after the generic C.36 question is recognized.

The truthful subject branch permits learning from candidate and source-described practice without laundering Method status. Separating carriers, authority, cultural claim, capability consequence, rival hypotheses, and causal reliance lets a team improve one relation while leaving unproved links open.

### HCD.17:11 - SoTA-Echoing

**Working question:** how should providers and practitioners deliberately continue or change one HCD cultural relation without treating a school, carrier, event, or formal adoption as the culture?

**Selected line — adopt and adapt.** Current C.36 supplies the best transdisciplinary problem answer: begin with a bounded collective or Discipline activity, variants and actual transmission, recognition, selection, memory or mediation relations, then change only the relation for which a proposal, Work, evidence and authority are current. Singh and colleagues' [review beyond “social learning”](https://doi.org/10.1098/rstb.2020.0050) is the best-known direct source candidate for the HCD evidence split because it distinguishes several transmission processes and warns that individual acquisition does not prove population continuation. HCD.17 adapts this line in §§4.1–4.8 by adding truthful HCD Method/candidate branches, provider and learner Work, separate human-capability consequence, and an authorized discriminating intervention. The review supplies no HCD intervention recipe, population capability, or E27 result.

The serious default is rollout culture: publish a curriculum or standard, train people, count participation, and call later activity adoption. It is administratively cheap but cannot distinguish carrier presence, receiving use, recognition, selection, memory, retention, capability, or causal effect. HCD.17 rejects that compression while retaining each supported publication, institutional-selection, teaching, participation, or community fact under its actual boundary.

Abedini, Abedin, and Zowghi's [review of adult online communities of practice](https://doi.org/10.1111/bjet.13120) and Brooks and colleagues' [community-support study](https://doi.org/10.1002/lrh2.10412) support facilitation, platform, coordination, access, experience exchange and participation as candidate contributions. They do not establish capability gain or cultural continuation. The [CDIO version history](https://cdio.org/content/version-history-and-references) is a necessary explicit comparator for formal generation and institutional-selection claims; official status supplies no evidence of receiving enactment, population retention, or effectiveness. These limits change §§4.3–4.8 and the countercases in §5.

Current ME.17 is the strongest nearby reusable Method but governs Method-Engineering culture. HCD.17 consumes its status, predicate, hypothesis, authorization and interval discipline while retaining the HCD-specific action and consequence. OCE.10 governs organization-side participation and target working culture when organization change is current. The selected HCD line costs more than label counting but is better on falsifiability, local authority, harm prevention, and truthful capability boundaries.

Reopen the comparison when a direct source changes the relevant transmission or community claim, when stronger HCD cultural-intervention evidence changes the predicate or probe, when the population, Method status, provider, support, interval, authority, intended consequence, or serious alternative changes, or when another practice returns the same truth and repair value at lower effort.

### HCD.17:12 - Relations

HCD.14 may supply a compatible revision hypothesis as one proposed cultural variation. HCD.16 may supply a bounded repertoire disposition or status-preserved candidate variation. Neither result proves generation, transmission, receiving enactment, recognition, selection, memory, retention, loss, or intervention authority. Equivalent direct evidence permits independent entry.

HCD.11, HCD.12, and HCD.13 or qualified equivalents govern the separate human-capability, unfamiliar-transfer, delayed-performance, and support-dependence observations. HCD.17 does not infer them from a cultural predicate.

C.36 governs the generic cultural-evolution question and relation boundary. HCD.17 supplies the HCD provider, learner and practitioner population, actual development Work or truthful candidate branch, intended human capability and later-Work consequence, domain intervention, and evidence return.

ME.17 governs Method-Engineering culture, including its admitted-Method and candidate branches for that Discipline. HCD.17 does not copy ME culture by changing the domain noun; it uses the same status and evidence discipline for a different working problem and first result. Method admission, descriptions, variants, provenance, introduction, fit, worth and generic currentness remain with ME.

OCE.10 governs participation and target working culture when the organizational System and organization-side change are current. OCE.17 governs Organization Change Engineering culture. Learning Product owns curriculum and carrier design. Clinical, employment, safety, legal, provider, platform, publication and target-domain owners retain their Methods, Work, service, evidence and authority.

### HCD.17:End


# Cross-Pattern Application

## APP-HCD-01 - Constructed ImpactReview-17 decision

`ImpactReview-17` is a constructed pedagogical application. Its answer key, `ImpactReview-17-Domain-Oracle-v1`, supplies engineering correctness and criticality criteria for `PumpWorks-Config-Baseline-42` and `AI-Assist-M4`. Its observations and decision conditions are stipulated so that the reader can follow demand derivation, diagnosis, and profile comparison. Operational use needs qualified criteria and evidence for the actual person and Work.

The exact human holder is Engineer-P14. The later Work is independent review of AI-proposed change-impact claims for mixed physical/software pump-controller changes before separate integration, assurance, and release decisions. The constructed 2026-Q4 conditions include current sources and configuration, a forty-five-minute case limit, the named AI assistant, recoverable audit records, and bounded safety and maintenance specialist returns. Final integration, safety, release, and employment authority remain elsewhere.

`HCD.1` uses eight stipulated representative review occurrences—four familiar and four unfamiliar, four with plausible wrong AI dependency claims, two needing specialist return—to return qualified demand only inside the constructed case. Engineer-P14 must recognize applicability, trace claims to source and configuration, independently challenge plausible AI output, delimit specialist results, return auditable evidence, and detect every seeded safety-critical omission under the protected conditions.

The constructed baseline has correct trace and evidence return in 8/8 cases, correct specialist routing in 2/2 relevant cases, and rejection of 2/4 plausible wrong AI claims. In the two unfamiliar misses, a neutral `E.23.CAE`-shaped probe preserves Method, source, tool, time, assignment, authority, and specialist route. Engineer-P14 recognizes the need for independent checking but does not generate and test a competing configuration hypothesis. `HCD.3` returns the qualified target **generate and test an independent configuration counter-hypothesis before accepting plausible AI output in unfamiliar variants**, with the small sample and live prompt-design rival explicit.

`HCD.4` keeps four rows:

| Claim | Current constructed evidence | Target and trade-off |
| --- | --- | --- |
| source/configuration trace | 8/8 | Preserve; do not trade traceability for speed. |
| independent AI-output challenge | 2/4 planted-error cases; both misses unfamiliar | Priority gap; target 4/4 and zero accepted seeded safety-critical omissions, with the forty-five-minute limit visible. |
| bounded specialist return | 2/2 relevant cases | Preserve the authority boundary. |
| auditable evidence return | 8/8 | Preserve; no aggregate may hide a missing critical-error record. |

Engineer-P14 is the person-level `DecisionSubject` for one self-development-focus choice inside a separately pre-authorized twelve-hour allocation. The pre-existing options are: `O1` preserve without selecting change; `O2` prioritize independent AI-output challenge for later programme comparison while preserving the supported claims; and `O3` defer a human priority and return the prompt-design question to the Method/tool owner.

The shared evidential comparison retains the small sample and prompt rival. Use two further unfamiliar seeded-error cases, one first attempt per case: a neutral prompt on one, an explicit counter-hypothesis prompt on the other. Keep the same sources, tools, specialist routes, and forty-five-minute limit per case. Up to ninety minutes for attempts and thirty minutes for preparation and comparison fit the two-hour probe budget inside the twelve-hour allocation. The observations can still change the `O2`-versus-`O3` comparison, but differences between the cases remain unresolved; this is not an isolated causal prompt test. The probe is feasible and worth its cost, so the explicit `C.11` result is **`ChoiceResult = probe again`**. No profile option or programme is selected. The separately named integration, safety, release, and employment decisions remain with their authorized owners.

## APP-HCD-02 - Constructed performance, transfer, retention and revision

Learner-L17 is a new fictional holder, distinct from Engineer-P14 and Engineer-E27. All attempts in this example are constructed observation traces, not data collected from programme learners.

The engineering exercise requires HW3/FW8. A report for FW7 cannot justify B's use for FW8 even though B has the highest stipulated NPV: A/B/C are approximately 48.9/51.6/44.6 thousand under the financial assumptions. A still needs a shared-power check. C with a compatible HW3/FW8/SCHED2 report preserves two hours of required checks per change and one hour of preparation per homogeneous batch of up to four. Ten changes take 23 hours; twelve take 27. At 24 available hours, a ten-change proposal leaves two of twelve new changes in the weekly queue. At 22 hours, nine take 21 and ten cannot be promised. The release manager retains the release/use decision. These are exercise predicates, not current engineering or safety guidance.

| Independent Method question | Constructed observation or proposed change | Bounded result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| HCD.11: what did L17 contribute? | The first answer calculates correctly but accepts B despite FW7. A teacher cue produces correction. In two later fresh representative cases, source and assistant traces show L17 recognizing the mismatch before any teacher or AI cue, then explaining a supported continuation and queue limit. | Initial compatibility failure, helped correction and later independent performance remain distinct; the sample supports only the tested contribution and conditions. |
| HCD.12: what survives unfamiliarity? | A complete HW3/FW8/SCHED2 report now supports action. L17 independently proposes ten at 24 hours and states the queue limit. At 22 hours, L17 first keeps ten and corrects to nine only after feedback. | Configuration-evidence transfer is supported in that case; independent resource adaptation is not. A fresh uncued variation is needed. |
| HCD.14: which arrangement component changes now? | At the transfer return, replace the cue-led resource exercise with action/refusal contrasts and a fresh uncued capacity variation. Keep calculation, configuration checking, source access and resource limits protected. | A local testable revision hypothesis, not a settled cause, performed programme change or causal effect. HCD.13 is not a prerequisite. |
| HCD.13: what remains after the relevant interval? | Matched earlier and eight-week observations cover selected F.1, C.16 and C.11 contributions under stated intermittent practice and normal support. A separately specified fifteen-minute core uses tables and calculator but no AI; task order remains visible. A further source-access failure is returned as such. | Retention of the compared contributions and the narrow core under their conditions; no claim for untested patterns, non-use, the whole cohort or the revision's causal effect. |

The bodies contain their own decisive facts and observations, so any of these questions can be used without reconstructing this connected account. The L17 example supplies none of the missing same-holder observations in ReleaseWork-R9 below.

## APP-HCD-03 - Constructed continuation, Method repertoire and culture

Engineer-E27, `CapabilityDevelopmentTeam-E27`, `ReleaseWork-R9`, and every development and cultural observation in this application are constructed. The application does not establish that the later Work occurred, a provider operated, E27 participated, a Method was admitted or enacted, capability changed, or a cultural relation obtained.

The intended later-Work family is independent review of AI-assisted mixed physical/software release claims, including configuration impact, challenge of plausible wrong AI output, auditable evidence return, justified action or refusal, and bounded safety and maintenance specialist use. The example stipulates one compatible HCD.14 input: preserve familiar trace and specialist-return practice while replacing one cue-led correction exercise with action/refusal contrasts and one fresh uncued counter-hypothesis case.

| Independent Method question | Constructed first result | Boundary preserved |
| --- | --- | --- |
| HCD.15: what keeps development responsive? | A continuing arrangement reopens after a material release-Method, configuration, AI, critical-error, qualification, source, provider or support change. It supplies representative cases, first-attempt capture, current sources, permitted AI conditions, coaching and specialist access, reassessment, burden limits, owners and stops. | A trigger or plan is not performed development Work, later Work, participation, achieved capability or causal effect. |
| HCD.16: which repertoire claim changes? | Preserve the admitted trace-and-specialist-return Method only under the constructed status and conditions; retire “assisted practice accuracy demonstrates independent learning” for this use; keep cue-led and counter-hypothesis-first candidate accounts as candidates; return `further probe` for matched unfamiliar cases. | The HCD disposition neither admits nor universally retires a Method, creates a variant, proves fit or worth, or transfers AI findings from customer support, consulting or school mathematics to release engineering. |
| HCD.17: which cultural relation may be changed? | Bound the population to one provider slice and assigned practitioners in 2026-Q4; test receiving use of a counter-hypothesis before a cue supplies it; compare carrier-sufficient and coached-feedback hypotheses; return `branch` with one fresh pre/post-coaching observation and a burden check. | Candidate use is not Method enactment. The branch establishes no organization- or field-wide adoption, long-term retention, ReleaseWork-R9 consequence, capability change or causal effect. |

The four direct relations remain conditional. The HCD.14 hypothesis can inform HCD.15 or HCD.17; observed HCD.15 use can inform HCD.16; and an HCD.16 disposition can inform HCD.17. A receiver must still verify holder or population, later Work, status, conditions, evidence window and authority. A stale, differently scoped or absent result is replaced only by a qualified direct substitute or an exact missing-result return, never by PatternID order.

The application deliberately leaves the current ReleaseWork-R9 missing-value table below unchanged. It demonstrates how the three Methods return useful bounded results when their case inputs are stipulated, not how to turn those stipulated values into evidence about Engineer-E27.


## Application return to a programme and instrument designer

The specified FoundationsProgramme is a 34-week design with 340 learner hours, 240 teaching hours, 16 topics and 49 practiced patterns. Those are design quantities, not observed learner outcomes. HCD can qualify programme, practice and evidence questions; the Learning Product owner still designs and resources the actual programme, tasks and instrument.

Before particular learners are known, the present decision concerns a learning product for a future audience. HCD.2 therefore returns the design values or candidate fragments needed for that product rather than inventing a personal recommendation. PSD can compare the author's A/B/C learning-product options. Once one person becomes current, a personal HCD.2 comparison needs that same holder's later Work, qualified target or profile, starting attempt, support conditions, feasible candidates and finite resources. It still stops before programme choice, commitment or launch.

At the original ten learner-hours per week, the three A/B/C compositions are 340-hour candidates. If participant time falls to six hours a week, the 34-week limit becomes 204 hours. All three original designs then require revision. The author must identify which 136 hours are removed or replaced, what complete-task practice and evidence are preserved, and whether both intended outcomes remain attainable. The three over-budget candidates do not establish that a suitable 204-hour programme exists.

This is a constraint on the whole proposed combination. In the original design, A allocates 202/96/42 learner hours to general/domain/joint application, B allocates 88/204/48, and C allocates 136/136/68; each totals 340. The usefulness of an individual topic, exercise or pair of contributions does not establish that all selected contributions fit the same learner's 204 hours. Each revised candidate must account once for its complete learner effort and show that the required preparation, whole-task attempts, feedback, correction and evidence can coexist within that limit. The teacher's separate contribution has its own limit and timing test below.

HCD.2's useful immediate return is a comparable fragment for one complete task family. The author can retain C's available detail and develop the smallest missing A/B fragment with the same output, audience assumptions, practice, help and evidence opportunity. HCD.5/.6 explain the different development contribution; HCD.7 tests the corresponding provider demand; HCD.8 enters only for an exact gap that must be obtained or built. The resulting comparison can expose a useful substitution or a remaining constraint. It still leaves the existence of a full 204-hour programme open. Changing an intended output is a separate target decision, as illustrated by HCD.2:5.2's narrower 170-hour offer.


Teaching capacity is another contribution account. For twelve participants, `32 × 6.5 + 2 × 16 = 240` teaching hours and `240 / 12 = 20` hours per participant are planning allocations, not twenty hours of individual teacher contact. HCD.7 must establish the actual modelling, feedback, assessment, specialist, tool, AI and checking contributions at their working times and peak load. With twenty-four participants, the same per-person checking demand either doubles the checking work or requires an explicit split or changed service design; the 240-hour label cannot simply be reused.

The new programme-and-practice Methods make the design chain inspectable without turning it into a mandatory sequence. HCD.5 selects a Method from the target action, starting attempt, mechanism, task, provider, risk and evidence need. HCD.6 designs representative action-and-refusal contrasts, including compatible FW8 evidence and 24- versus 22-hour resource cases where the changed limit should change the answer. HCD.7 establishes the sources, tables, calculator, AI conditions, assessor competence, capacity, human checking and fallback. HCD.8 is used only if one exact contribution remains missing and obtaining or building it has to be decided.

For example, assessor preparation can use three contrasting answers: a supported ten-change proposal under 24 hours, an unsupported twelve-change promise under the same limit, and a justified request when the firmware evidence is absent. Independent judgements and criterion-level disagreement resolution are preparation. Consistent and timely judgement on a changed 22-hour case is the bounded operating test; it does not establish general assessor capability, whole-window service operation or a learner effect.

HCD.9 then keeps the learner's first attempt, actual help, criterion-bearing feedback, correction, targeted retry and next focus separate. HCD.10 can vary a decision-bearing condition, return to the target after an interval, interleave a competing Method family, or change support or challenge. None of these designs by itself establishes improved capability, transfer, retention or causal effectiveness.

Its planned delayed visits concern six selected patterns: F.1, F.0.1, C.16, B.5.2, A.10.1 and C.11.

| Planned visit | Selected contribution | Conditions that affect the interpretation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Week 8 | F.1 and F.0.1, about six weeks after their initial work | Ordinary sources and tools; five minutes of independent question recognition before searching. |
| Week 17 | C.16 and B.5.2 in a different managerial or engineering case | Pattern texts and AI for the main task; a separate short internal-core probe. |
| Week 25 | F.1, C.16 and A.10.1 in a configuration question | Record use and practice during the intervening weeks rather than assuming non-use. |
| Week 34 | F.1, C.16 and C.11 in an integration case | Normal means for the main task and a separately specified first move without AI. |

Weeks 8, 17, 25 and 34 are an HCD.10 spacing design only because they are separated returns to selected contributions with intervening practice made visible. HCD.13 owns any later inference about retained performance or support dependence. Calendar dates alone establish neither retrieval nor retention.

The thirty-minute final core contains one fifteen-minute no-AI fragment; supplied tables and a calculator remain available. The main task retains ordinary pattern and AI access. The fragment tests recovery of the configuration and time constraints, rejection of an unsupported release, and a valid next proposal. It is not a compressed closed-book examination of the whole course. An additional six-to-eight-week follow-up outside the programme needs separately agreed time, assessor and access.

The proposed Q2 unfamiliar-Method instrument remains a distinct design task:

| Minutes | Intended observation |
| ---: | --- |
| 5 | Prior familiarity and relevant conditions. |
| 8 | Unannounced recognition and finding a usable source, without a PatternID cue. |
| 22 | First application after every participant receives the same exact new source. |
| 15 | Adaptation to a material changed condition. |
| 10 | Checking, correction and explanation, with first and helped performance kept separate. |

These sixty minutes replace the existing one-hour task; they do not add an hour to the 340-hour design. The designer must still show that the tasks fit. A proposed ME.14 task must test its new practical-disposition contribution against current alternatives; a SYSE.23 task must distinguish target-system change from enabling-platform change and test the anticipated future benefit. Familiar cost arithmetic or reused C.11 comparison alone cannot demonstrate acquisition of either whole contribution.

Retain recognition, first and corrected performance, new-source contribution, help, time, material errors and unfinished attempts separately. “Faster learning at unchanged correctness” needs actual comparable observations; different Methods at weeks 17 and 34 do not isolate a training effect. The three-to-five-hour instrument-authoring estimate is not the cost of a learner experiment. These specifications supply no Q2 result, pilot extension or actual learner trial.

## ReleaseWork-R9 - Prospective demand and discriminated missing evidence

For Engineer-E27, the accepted future architecture permits `HCD.1` to return a prospective demand hypothesis for AI-output challenge, configuration-impact reasoning, evidence return, specialist coordination, critical-error detection, and unfamiliar-variant adaptation in 2026-Q4 `ReleaseWork-R9`. It does not permit qualified current demand.

The missing values remain separate:

| Missing value | What it prevents now | Retry boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| compatible obtaining `SYSE.21` supplier result required by `XRI-21` | Any HCD.1 demand use that consumes current engineering-culture enactment, bounded practice-change decision, retained alternatives, or evidence gaps | Obtain that compatible result, or a receiver-permitted qualified direct substitute, for Engineer-E27, the Method, Work, period, and evidence use. |
| actual ReleaseWork-R9 Work occurrence | Treating ReleaseWork-R9 itself as current later-Work evidence | Admit performed Work with Engineer-E27, configuration, period, and performer recoverable. |
| occurrence-produced result or supported direct result claim | Any conclusion consuming the occurrence's obtained quality, error, result, or consequence | Establish the produced result or supported direct claim tied to the admitted occurrence. |
| occurrence-bound result record | Any record-dependent inference | Relate a current result episteme to the admitted occurrence and result; do not require it where qualified direct observations suffice. |
| representative occurrence-bound observations | Qualified current demand, a diagnostic target, and a current profile comparison | Obtain holder- and condition-compatible observations or a named qualified representative set covering performed Method, configuration, work products, quality and critical errors, AI/support posture, specialist returns, variants, and evidence window. |

The current `SYSE.21` MethodDescription and its constructed PumpWorks demonstration may shape questions but are not the missing `XRI-21` supplier result. The HCD carrier, accepted source decision, R9 synthesis, target repertoire, plan, curriculum, course completion, platform score, or orphan record cannot fill any row by label.

Current returns are therefore conclusion-specific: HCD.1 preserves the prospective demand hypothesis and returns `demand-evidence-blocked` for qualified current demand; HCD.3 returns `diagnostic-evidence-missing` and selects no training; HCD.4 retains only prospective target claims and returns `profile-decision-blocked`. Recovering one missing value changes only the conclusions that consume it.

## Clinical unlike case - Return diagnosis and treatment to their owner

Return an adult's request for treatment of social anxiety to qualified clinical care. HCD.1 may address a separately posed, legitimate question about non-clinical participation in later Work, supplied by the person or qualified clinical owner.

HCD.3 returns a non-training clinical route whenever diagnosis, treatment choice, exposure or CBT content, clinical safety, provider competence, consent, or outcome interpretation is current. HCD.4 does not turn symptom measures, treatment plans, or provider pathways into a mastery profile.

Qualified clinical professionals make judgements about diagnosis, treatment, provider competence, safety, and clinical outcomes. The person gives or withholds consent; other authorized actors make the decisions within their remit. HCD prescribes no exposure, behavioural experiment, homework, counterconditioning, symptom endpoint, or provider arrangement.

# Framework Boundary and Refresh

## Intended use and ordinary non-use

Use this first edition to derive or return a human demand question, identify a limiting target, compare a profile, compose and compare personal programme candidates, select a development Method, design representative practice, establish support, obtain or build one missing arrangement contribution, conduct and progress focused practice, assess a contribution, test a material unfamiliar variation, compare delayed and supported performance, revise a development assumption, configure continuing development, update one HCD Method-repertoire use, or deliberately continue and change one bounded HCD cultural relation.

Do not use it as a ready-made curriculum, whole provider service, complete assessment instrument, universal HCD Method repertoire, organization-change programme, or proof of cultural continuation. It supplies neither clinical treatment, specialist correctness, release or employment authority, nor evidence that a provider operated, development was performed, capability changed, or an intervention caused an effect.

## PatternID and reader order

HCD.* is the Human Capability Development PatternID namespace. Numbers are stable addresses, not steps. The reader order is HCD.1, HCD.3, HCD.4, HCD.2, HCD.5, HCD.6, HCD.7, HCD.8, HCD.9, HCD.10, HCD.11, HCD.12, HCD.13, HCD.14, HCD.15, HCD.16 and HCD.17. Demand is a useful cold start when the required human contribution is unknown; equivalent qualified input permits direct entry to any Method under its own conditions. Reader order is not programme order, Work order, package dependency or a mandatory lifecycle.

## Available patterns

| PatternID | Current reader availability | Honest return |
| --- | --- | --- |
| HCD.1 | Available: derive human capability demand from representative later Work. | Demand account, prospective hypothesis, outside-HCD return or discriminated evidence blocker. |
| HCD.3 | Available: diagnose a limiting capability, misconception or behaviour. | Qualified intervention target or non-training return. |
| HCD.4 | Available: architect a balanced profile across simultaneous Work. | Profile comparison, recommendation, unresolved question, bounded choice or exact blocker. |
| HCD.2 | Available: compose and compare capability-development programmes for later Work. | Same-holder programme comparison, exact missing input, or learning-product design return when the person is not yet known; no programme choice. |
| HCD.5 | Available: select a capability-development Method. | Bounded Method selection or shortlist, non-training return, or exact missing input; no effectiveness claim. |
| HCD.6 | Available: design representative practice tasks. | Criterion-bearing task set with whole action, support, critical errors, correction, meaningful variation and resource fit. |
| HCD.7 | Available: arrange providers, access, tools and AI support. | Enabled contribution arrangement with competence, access, capacity, checking and fallback, or the exact gap. |
| HCD.8 | Available: build the recursive capability-development arrangement. | Obtain/build decision, bounded provider or environment preparation, representative operating test, or first unsupported dependency. |
| HCD.9 | Available: perform deliberate practice with feedback. | Focused-practice episode preserving first attempt, help, feedback, correction, retry and next focus. |
| HCD.10 | Available: vary, space, interleave and progress practice. | Condition-sensitive next episodes with timing, retrieval, sources, support, challenge, observation and response rule kept distinct. |
| HCD.11 | Available: assess performance in representative Work. | Observed contribution and bounded capability inference, or a distinct evidence or assessment gap. |
| HCD.12 | Available: test transfer to unfamiliar Work. | Transferred contribution, adaptation need and tested limits, with new-source learning separate. |
| HCD.13 | Available: test retention and tool dependence. | Condition-qualified retention, independent-core and support-dependence findings or unresolved comparison. |
| HCD.14 | Available: revise the development arrangement from evidence. | Specific revision hypothesis, test, cost, protected conditions and conditional update. |
| HCD.15 | Available: sustain continuing Human Capability Development. | Holder-specific continuing arrangement with triggers, contributions, evidence, burden, authority, re-entry and stops, or an exact blocker. |
| HCD.16 | Available: develop and refresh HCD Methods. | Exact Method/candidate and claim disposition—`adopt`, `revise`, `branch`, `retire` or `further probe`—for one bounded repertoire use. |
| HCD.17 | Available: deliberately continue and change HCD culture. | Truthful enacted/candidate account, testable predicate, bounded intervention and `continue`, `revise`, `branch`, `replace`, `stop` or `unknown`. |

This complete first edition does not claim that any Method was applied, any provider operated, any person developed, any culture changed, or any particular result was achieved.

## Source use and currentness

The HCD.1/.3/.4 direct-source qualifications remain dated **1 September 2026**. The [NICE social-anxiety guideline](https://www.nice.org.uk/guidance/cg159/chapter/recommendations) (`BEH-05`) was separately checked on **31 August 2026**; refresh its affected clinical boundary when the recommendation changes.

`HCD.1` uses [Kraiger and Ford’s workplace-instruction review (2021)](https://doi.org/10.1146/annurev-orgpsych-012420-060109) (`COG-10`) for the event-to-transfer distinction, and Dell’Acqua and colleagues’ [Navigating the jagged technological frontier](https://doi.org/10.1287/orsc.2025.21838) (`AI-03`) for a bounded independent-error-challenge cue. HCD.1 also uses current FPF, qualified target-domain results, and evidence for the person's later Work.

`HCD.3` uses only the bounded diagnostic warning from Melanson and Fahmie’s [Functional analysis of problem behavior: a 40-year review](https://doi.org/10.1002/jaba.983) (2023; `BEH-01`), the clinical stop from NICE, and the target-engagement limits from Sheeran and colleagues’ [Self-determination theory interventions for health behavior change](https://doi.org/10.1037/ccp0000501) (2020; `MOT-01`) and [Does increasing autonomous motivation or perceived competence lead to health behavior change?](https://doi.org/10.1037/hea0001111) (2021; `MOT-02`). HCD.3 also uses `E.23.CAE` when its differential can change the decision, and direct specialist evidence when the question needs it. These health and clinical sources supply no universal human-capability diagnosis or clinical procedure for HCD.

`HCD.4` uses Winget and Persky’s [A practical review of mastery learning](https://doi.org/10.5688/ajpe8906) (2022; `COG-06`) for local thresholds, correction and retest; Gutierrez de Blume’s [Calibrating calibration](https://doi.org/10.1037/edu0000674) (2022; `COG-07`) for self-monitoring accuracy; Arieli-Attali and colleagues’ [Expanded evidence-centered design](https://doi.org/10.3389/fpsyg.2019.00853) (2019; `COG-08`) for claim/task/evidence/inference traceability; and Abdelrahman and colleagues’ [Knowledge tracing: a survey](https://doi.org/10.1145/3569576) (2023; `COG-09`) for model-prediction limits. The design framework is not a causal intervention study, and none of these sources establishes P14's current capability, a universal ladder, or transfer and retention. `C.32.MWA`, `C.11`, and direct task evidence supply the remaining architecture, choice, and local evidence conditions.

The HCD.2 and HCD.5–HCD.10 bodies bind their source uses to programme comparison, Method selection, task design, support, provider or environment operation, focused practice, and condition-sensitive progression. Their shared source line is summarized here; the individual bodies carry the decisive limits and reopen conditions.

| Source group | Contribution and limit used by HCD.2 and HCD.5–HCD.10 |
| --- | --- |
| Maintained [4C/ID model](https://www.4cid.org/about/) and Paas and van Merriënboer ([2020; `COG-05`](https://doi.org/10.1177/0963721420922183)) | Sufficiently whole complex tasks, integrated information, worked support and expertise-sensitive guidance; no local programme effect, mandatory sequence or universal fading rule. |
| Winget and Persky ([2022; `COG-06`](https://doi.org/10.5688/ajpe8906)) and Wisniewski, Zierer, and Hattie ([2020; `MOT-05`](https://doi.org/10.3389/fpsyg.2019.03087)) | Corrective activity, retest and information-bearing feedback under task and receiver conditions; no universal threshold, feedback type or later-work inference. |
| Carpenter, Pan, and Butler ([2022; `COG-01`](https://doi.org/10.1038/s44159-022-00089-1)), Brunmair and Richter ([2019; `COG-03`](https://doi.org/10.1037/bul0000209)), and Czyż, Wójcik, and Solarská ([2024; `COG-04`](https://doi.org/10.3389/fpsyg.2024.1377122)) | Spacing, retrieval, interleaving and delayed-transfer candidates whose value depends on material, similarity, setting and horizon; no universal gap, mix, repetition count or professional schedule. |
| Kraiger and Ford ([2021; `COG-10`](https://doi.org/10.1146/annurev-orgpsych-012420-060109)), Matsuo and Tsukube ([2020; `SOC-02`](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ijme.2020.100417)), Brooks and colleagues ([2024; `SOC-05`](https://doi.org/10.1002/lrh2.10412)), and Wiese, Kilty, and Bennett ([2018; `SOC-03`](https://doi.org/10.1111/medu.13655)) | Separation of instructional events, learner activity, provider contributions, access, supervision, trust, risk and service pressure; no generic provider credential, capability effect or transplanted clinical governance. |
| Tullis, Goldstone, and Hanson ([2015; `COG-11`](https://doi.org/10.1080/00222895.2015.1008686)), Dell’Acqua and colleagues ([`AI-03`](https://doi.org/10.1287/orsc.2025.21838)), and Bastani and colleagues ([2025; `AI-04`](https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.2422633122)) | Assisted and later unassisted performance, human checking, and AI-support conditions remain distinct; laboratory, consulting-task and school-mathematics results establish neither a universal support schedule nor an adult workplace effect or AI ban. |
| Macnamara, Hambrick, and Oswald ([2014; `BEH-02`](https://doi.org/10.1177/0956797614535810)), Nurse and colleagues ([2024; `BEH-03`](https://doi.org/10.1080/10503307.2024.2308159)), and Sheeran and colleagues ([2020; `MOT-01`](https://doi.org/10.1037/ccp0000501), [2024; `MOT-03`](https://doi.org/10.1080/10463283.2024.2334563)) | Limits on universal deliberate-practice doses, bounded focused-practice ingredients, target engagement, and conditional if–then planning; no fixed hours threshold, cross-domain causality or professional transfer guarantee. |

The added HCD.11–HCD.14 source uses were qualified on **3 September 2026**. The bodies bind each source to a specific assessment or revision rule:

| Source | Contribution and limit used here |
| --- | --- |
| [2014 Testing Standards, Chapter 1](https://www.testingstandards.net/uploads/7/6/6/4/76643089/9780935302356.pdf) | Intended assessment interpretation and use, task fitness and scoring evidence; design guidance, not local validation or authorization. |
| [Arieli-Attali and colleagues, expanded evidence-centred design, 2019](https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/psychology/articles/10.3389/fpsyg.2019.00853/full) | Claim/task/observation/inference and learning during assessment; no treatment effect is inferred. |
| [Kraiger and Ford, workplace instruction, 2021](https://www.annualreviews.org/content/journals/10.1146/annurev-orgpsych-012420-060109) | Instruction, learner activity, learning and work transfer remain distinct; no particular programme's effectiveness is established. |
| [Carpenter, Pan and Butler, 2022](https://www.nature.com/articles/s44159-022-00089-1) and [Yang and colleagues, 2021](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/33683913/) | Retrieval/testing can affect learning under specified conditions; no universal delay, engineer effect size or all-domain transfer guarantee. |
| [Bastani and colleagues, 2025](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/40560616/) | Assisted practice and later unassisted performance can differ; school-mathematics evidence is not adult occupational transfer or long retention. |
| [Schwartz and Martin, 2004](https://aaalab.stanford.edu/assets/papers/2004/Inventing_to_prepare_for_future_learning.pdf) | Historical design evidence for distinguishing learning with a new resource from performance without it; not a claim that this programme accelerates learning. |

The HCD.15–HCD.17 applied-learning source uses were qualified on **1 September 2026**. The cultural-continuation comparison was checked on **24 August 2026**. The new bodies use only claims that change their practitioner move:

| Source group | Contribution and limit used by HCD.15–HCD.17 |
| --- | --- |
| Kraiger and Ford ([2021; `COG-10`](https://doi.org/10.1146/annurev-orgpsych-012420-060109)); Matsuo and Tsukube ([2020; `SOC-02`](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ijme.2020.100417)); Wiese, Kilty, and Bennett ([2018; `SOC-03`](https://doi.org/10.1111/medu.13655)) | HCD.15 separates instructional events, learner activity, learning outcomes, transfer and job performance and may use bounded modeling, coaching, supervision and graduated access. No universal cadence, provider form, transplanted clinical governance or E27 trajectory follows. |

Membership and activity establish neither capability, cultural continuation nor causal effect. |
| Brynjolfsson, Li, and Raymond ([2025; `AI-02`](https://doi.org/10.1093/qje/qjae044)); Dell'Acqua and colleagues ([2026; `AI-03`](https://doi.org/10.1287/orsc.2025.21838)); Bastani and colleagues ([2025; `AI-04`](https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.2422633122)) | HCD.16 separates deployed equipped performance, rare-outage same-job signals, task-frontier error, assisted practice and later unassisted performance. These populations and tasks do not establish an adult release-engineering Method, general AI benefit or harm, other-Work transfer, or long retention. |

| Singh and colleagues ([2021; `SOC-06`](https://doi.org/10.1098/rstb.2020.0050)) through current C.36 | HCD.17 distinguishes generation, transmission, receiving use, recognition, selection, memory, retention and loss. The review supplies no HCD intervention recipe, authority or population capability. |
| [`SRC-DOMAIN-CULTURAL-CONTINUATION-CASES-2001-2026`](https://github.com/ailev/FPF/blob/main/_change-campaigns/dpf-systems-modeling-systems-engineering-systems-management-operations-management/DPF-ARCHITECTURE-CAMPAIGN/comparison/SOURCE-REGISTRY.md#src-domain-cultural-continuation-cases-2001-2026), including [CDIO version history](https://cdio.org/content/version-history-and-references) | Formal generation, edition, institutional selection, carrier and reported-use claims may be stated at their evidence boundary. They do not establish provider or learner enactment, population retention, improved capability or effectiveness. |


The programme and L17 engineering exercise supply designed tasks, stipulated domain facts and constructed observations, not an empirical learner dataset. Real engineering, safety or professional claims require current direct-domain sources and actual case evidence.

`ImpactReview-17-Domain-Oracle-v1` is constructed case data, not a source-ledger entry or currentness claim. Replacing any stipulated engineering correctness or criticality value with a real-world claim requires a named current target-domain Method or result and qualified safety or specialist source.

Refresh only the affected pattern or case claim when a relied-on FPF distinction, direct source contract, target-domain Method or result, specialist boundary, holder, Work family, configuration, evidence window, critical-error predicate, case truth posture, support arrangement, practice condition, or receiving decision changes. A new publication alone does not reopen unaffected claims.

## FPF dependency and compatibility

This first edition depends on **First Principles Framework (FPF) — Core Conceptual Specification, Version August 2026**, status **Normative kernel, eternal alpha**, at the current-pattern state qualified for this product.

FPF supplies System and holder recognition, capability, Method and MethodDescription, Work occurrence and result distinctions, evidence and uncertainty, architecture, agency, decision objects, authority, cultural relations, and precise wording. HCD supplies human-demand, target-or-return, balanced-profile, programme-comparison, development-Method selection, representative-practice, support-arrangement, bounded provider-development, focused-practice, progression, performance-assessment, transfer, retention, evidence-led revision, continuing-development arrangement, bounded HCD Method-repertoire, and HCD cultural-continuation Methods for its domain.

`E.23.CDI` governs the independent-System holder condition and later capability-development evidence under its own entry conditions. `E.23.CAE` supplies an observation-first differential where applicability, access, adaptation, enactment, and actual capability change must be separated. `C.32.MWA` prevents source layouts from becoming profile architecture. `C.11.CRC` governs finite comparison inside HCD.2, while `C.11` governs a bounded current-option choice. Compatible FPF changes leave unaffected HCD results reusable; changed relied-on distinctions reopen only their consumers.

## External result and authority boundaries

Target-domain Methods and results govern what counts as correct, safe, critical, or useful in later Work. Clinical, safety, legal, employment, assignment, organization, provider, platform, tool, and specialist owners retain their direct results and authority. HCD may consume those results or return an exact missing value; it does not replace them.

An assisting AI or other System may contribute Work or evidence when its capability, access, configuration, and authority obtain. Its output is not human capability, and it is not the `DecisionSubject` by label. Managers may own allocation and assignment while the human holder participates and while programme, safety, release, clinical, or employment decisions stay separate.

## Edition return

**Human Capability Development Principles Framework — Seventeen-pattern first edition, 5 September 2026** designates this Readme source, its Table of Contents and presentation carrier, and exactly seventeen authoritative bodies in reader order: HCD.1, HCD.3, HCD.4, HCD.2, HCD.5, HCD.6, HCD.7, HCD.8, HCD.9, HCD.10, HCD.11, HCD.12, HCD.13, HCD.14, HCD.15, HCD.16 and HCD.17.

The designation identifies the complete HCD pattern set and reader carrier. The Engineering DPF Suite includes this HCD framework alongside its other DPFs and common foundations. Actual use, provider operation, performed development Work, achieved capability, cultural continuation and causal effectiveness require the corresponding observations and decisions described here.

**Description:** HCD is a pattern language for deriving human capability demand from later Work, designing and sustaining development arrangements, and judging human contribution, transfer and retention under stated task, support and time conditions.

**Author:** Anatoly Levenchuk with AI-assisted development and review.

**Version:** 5 September 2026

**Status:** Eternal alpha: used in working development projects and open to continuing correction.

**Publication:** [FPF repository](https://github.com/ailev/FPF).

Eternal alpha identifies a framework open to continuing correction and development. Individual bodies retain their declared status and claim boundaries. The publication supplies practitioner guidance; evidence for its use in a particular person, provider, programme or population is established at that use.

## Citation

Levenchuk, Anatoly, with AI-assisted development and review. *Human Capability Development Principles Framework*. Version: 5 September 2026. [FPF repository](https://github.com/ailev/FPF).

For a particular pattern, name the framework and PatternID; include the version date above when the citation must recover the body published in this version.
